// lib/admin.ts Ã¢â‚¬â€ server-side admin fetchers with cookie forwarding.

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

async function adminFetch<T>(path: string): Promise<T> {
  const url = `${API}${path}`;

  // On the server (Server Component / layout), credentials:"include" does
  // nothing Ã¢â‚¬â€ Node's fetch has no cookie jar. We must forward the incoming
  // request's cookies as a Cookie header.
  const isServer = typeof window === "undefined";
  let cookieHeader: string | null = null;

  if (isServer) {
    const { cookies } = await import("next/headers");
    const store = await cookies(); // async in Next.js 15+
    cookieHeader = store
      .getAll()
      .map((c) => `${c.name}=${c.value}`)
      .join("; ");
  }

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: "GET",
    credentials: isServer ? "omit" : "include",
    headers: cookieHeader ? { cookie: cookieHeader } : undefined,
    cache: "no-store",
  });

  if (res.status === 401) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unauthorized Ã¢â‚¬â€ ted_session cookie not present in request to ${path}`
    );
  }
  if (res.status === 403) {
    throw new Error(`Forbidden Ã¢â‚¬â€ user is not an ADMIN (${path})`);
  }
  if (!res.ok) {
    const body = await res.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`${res.status} Ã¢â‚¬â€ ${path} Ã¢â‚¬â€ ${body.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  return (await res.json()) as T;
}

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Shape mirrors Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬

FILE: lib\api-client.ts
════════════════════════════════════════════
import { env } from "./env";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public status: number,
    public body?: unknown,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}

type RequestOptions = Omit<RequestInit, "body"> & { body?: unknown };

export async function apiFetch<T>(
  path: string,
  options: RequestOptions = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const url = `${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000"}${path}`;

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    ...options,
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      ...(options.headers ?? {}),
    },
    body: options.body ? JSON.stringify(options.body) : undefined,
    credentials: "include",
  });

  const text = await res.text();
  const data = text ? safeJson(text) : null;

  if (!res.ok) {
    throw new ApiError(
      (data as { message?: string })?.message ??
        `Request failed: ${res.status}`,
      res.status,
      data,
    );
  }

  return data as T;
}

function safeJson(text: string): unknown {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    return text;
  }
}

"use client";

// lib/cart.ts
// Thin adapter over @/modules/cart/service.
// This file exists only because checkout imports `useCart` from here.
// Do NOT add a second storage key â€” the service owns ted-nomex:cart.

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { Product } from "@/modules/shop/products/service";
import {
  getCart,
  addToCart as svcAdd,
  removeFromCart as svcRemove,
  updateQuantity as svcUpdate,
  clearCart as svcClear,
  subscribeToCart,
  type CartItem as ServiceCartItem,
} from "@/modules/cart/service";

export type CartItem = {
  productId: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  image: string | null;
  quantity: number;
};

function toPublic(item: ServiceCartItem): CartItem {
  return {
    productId: item.productId,
    slug: item.slug,
    name: item.name,
    price: Number(item.price),
    currency: item.currency,
    image: item.image,
    quantity: item.quantity,
  };
}

export function useCart() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<CartItem[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const refresh = () => setItems(getCart().map(toPublic));
    refresh();
    const unsub = subscribeToCart(refresh);
    return unsub;
  }, []);

  const addToCart = (product: Product, quantity = 1) => {
    svcAdd(
      {
        productId: product.id,
        slug: product.slug,
        name: product.name,
        price: Number(product.price),
        currency: product.currency ?? "GHS",
        image: product.images?.[0] ?? null,
        stock: product.stock,
      },
      quantity
    );
  };

  const removeFromCart = (productId: string) => {
    svcRemove(productId);
  };

  const setQuantity = (productId: string, qty: number) => {
    svcUpdate(productId, qty);
  };

  const clear = () => svcClear();

  const count = items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.quantity, 0);
  const subtotal = items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.price * i.quantity, 0);

  return {
    items,
    addToCart,
    removeFromCart,
    setQuantity,
    clear,
    count,
    subtotal,
  };
}

  NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL,

import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api-client";

export type SessionUser = {
  id: string;
  name: string | null;
  email: string;
  role: "CUSTOMER" | "ADMIN";
};

export async function getSession(): Promise<SessionUser | null> {
  try { return await apiFetch<SessionUser>("/api/auth/session"); }
  catch { return null; }
}

export async function login(email: string, password: string) {
  return apiFetch<{ user: SessionUser }>("/api/auth/login", {
    method: "POST",
    body: { email, password },
  });
}

export async function register(name: string, email: string, password: string) {
  return apiFetch<{ user: SessionUser }>("/api/auth/register", {
    method: "POST",
    body: { name, email, password },
  });
}

export async function logout() {
  return apiFetch<{ ok: true }>("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" });
}

// modules/auth/session.ts Ã¢â‚¬â€ server-safe auth client.

export type SessionRole = "CUSTOMER" | "ADMIN";

export type SessionUser = {
  id: string;
  name: string | null;
  email: string;
  role: SessionRole;
  image: string | null;
  phone: string | null;
  emailVerified: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
};

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

async function parse(res: Response) {
  let body: any = null;
  try { body = await res.json(); } catch { body = null; }
  if (!res.ok) {
    const msg = body?.message ?? body?.error ?? `Request failed (${res.status})`;
    const err = new Error(msg) as Error & { code?: string; status?: number };
    err.code = body?.code ?? body?.errorCode;
    err.status = res.status;
    throw err;
  }
  return body;
}

export function notifyAuthChanged() {
  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event("ted-auth-changed"));
  }
}

/**
 * getSession Ã¢â‚¬â€ works in BOTH Server and Client components.
 *
 * On the server: reads the incoming request's cookies via next/headers
 * and forwards them to the backend so /api/auth/session sees ted_session.
 *
 * On the client: normal fetch with credentials: "include".
 */
export async function getSession(): Promise<SessionUser | null> {
  try {
    // Detect server vs client
    const isServer = typeof window === "undefined";

    let cookieHeader: string | null = null;
    if (isServer) {
      // Dynamic import keeps this file importable from Client Components too
      const { cookies } = await import("next/headers");
      const store = await cookies(); // async in Next 15/16
      const all = store.getAll();
      cookieHeader = all.map((c) => `${c.name}=${c.value}`).join("; ");
    }

    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/auth/session`, {
      method: "GET",
      credentials: isServer ? "omit" : "include",
      headers: cookieHeader ? { cookie: cookieHeader } : undefined,
      cache: "no-store",
    });

    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const data = await res.json();
    return data?.user ?? null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Login Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
export async function login(email: string, password: string): Promise<SessionUser> {
  const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/auth/login`, {
    method: "POST",
    credentials: "include",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
  });
  const data = await parse(res);
  notifyAuthChanged();
  return data.user as SessionUser;
}

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Register Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
export async function register(
  name: string,
  email: string,
  password: string,
  phone?: string | null,
): Promise<SessionUser> {
  const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/auth/register`, {
    method: "POST",
    credentials: "include",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ name, email, password, phone: phone ?? undefined }),
  });
  const data = await parse(res);
  notifyAuthChanged();
  return data.user as SessionUser;
}

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Logout Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
export async function logout(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await fetch(`${API}/api/auth/logout`, {
      method: "POST",
      credentials: "include",
    });
  } catch {}
  notifyAuthChanged();
}

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Google OAuth Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬

FILE: modules\auth\useSession.ts
════════════════════════════════════════════
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { getSession, type SessionUser } from "@/modules/auth/session";

export function useSession() {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<SessionUser | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;

    (async () => {
      const u = await getSession();
      if (alive) {
        setUser(u);
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();

    const refresh = async () => {
      const u = await getSession();
      if (alive) setUser(u);
    };

    window.addEventListener("ted-auth-changed", refresh);
    window.addEventListener("storage", refresh);

    return () => {
      alive = false;
      window.removeEventListener("ted-auth-changed", refresh);
      window.removeEventListener("storage", refresh);
    };
  }, []);

  return { user, loading };
}

// modules/cart/service.ts
"use client";

export type CartItem = {
  productId: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  image: string | null;
  quantity: number;
  stock: number;
};

const STORAGE_KEY = "ted-nomex:cart";

export function getCart(): CartItem[] {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as unknown;
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
    return parsed as CartItem[];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function getCartCount(): number {
  return getCart().reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
}

function save(cart: CartItem[]) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cart));
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event("cart-changed"));
}

export function addToCart(item: Omit<CartItem, "quantity">, quantity = 1) {
  const cart = getCart();
  const existing = cart.find((i) => i.productId === item.productId);
  if (existing) {
    existing.quantity = Math.min(item.stock, existing.quantity + quantity);
  } else {
    cart.push({ ...item, quantity: Math.min(item.stock, quantity) });
  }
  save(cart);
}

export function updateQuantity(productId: string, quantity: number) {
  const cart = getCart();
  const item = cart.find((i) => i.productId === productId);
  if (!item) return;
  item.quantity = Math.max(1, Math.min(item.stock, quantity));
  save(cart);
}

export function removeFromCart(productId: string) {
  save(getCart().filter((i) => i.productId !== productId));
}

export function clearCart() {
  save([]);
}

export function subscribeToCart(callback: () => void): () => void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return () => {};
  const onChange = () => callback();
  window.addEventListener("cart-changed", onChange);
  window.addEventListener("storage", onChange);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener("cart-changed", onChange);
    window.removeEventListener("storage", onChange);
  };
}

// modules/cms/news/service.ts
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api-client";

export type NewsPost = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  excerpt: string;
  content: string;
  coverImage: string;
  category: string;
  published: boolean;
  publishedAt: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
};

export async function listNews(category?: string): Promise<NewsPost[]> {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams({ published: "true" });
  if (category) qs.set("category", category);
  return apiFetch<NewsPost[]>(`/api/cms/news?${qs}`);
}

export async function getNews(slug: string): Promise<NewsPost | null> {
  try { return await apiFetch<NewsPost>(`/api/cms/news/${slug}`); }
  catch { return null; }
}

export async function listAllNews(): Promise<NewsPost[]> {
  return apiFetch<NewsPost[]>("/api/cms/news/admin/all");
}

export async function getNewsById(id: string): Promise<NewsPost> {
  return apiFetch<NewsPost>(`/api/cms/news/admin/${id}`);
}

export async function createNews(input: {
  slug: string; title: string; excerpt: string; content: string;
  coverImage: string; category: string; published: boolean;
}): Promise<NewsPost> {
  return apiFetch<NewsPost>("/api/cms/news", { method: "POST", body: input });
}

export async function updateNews(id: string, input: Partial<{
  slug: string; title: string; excerpt: string; content: string;
  coverImage: string; category: string; published: boolean;
}>): Promise<NewsPost> {
  return apiFetch<NewsPost>(`/api/cms/news/${id}`, { method: "PATCH", body: input });
}

export async function deleteNews(id: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean }> {
  return apiFetch<{ ok: boolean }>(`/api/cms/news/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
}

// modules/cms/services/service.ts
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api-client";

export type Service = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  excerpt: string;
  content: string;
  coverImage: string;
  features: string[];
  tags: string[];
  bullets: string[];
  order: number;
  published: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
};

export async function listServices(): Promise<Service[]> {
  return apiFetch<Service[]>("/api/cms/services?published=true");
}

export async function getService(slug: string): Promise<Service | null> {
  try { return await apiFetch<Service>(`/api/cms/services/${slug}`); }
  catch { return null; }
}

export async function listAllServices(): Promise<Service[]> {
  return apiFetch<Service[]>("/api/cms/services/admin/all");
}

export async function getServiceById(id: string): Promise<Service> {
  return apiFetch<Service>(`/api/cms/services/admin/${id}`);
}

export type ServiceInput = {
  slug: string; title: string; excerpt: string; content: string;
  coverImage: string; features: string[]; tags: string[]; bullets: string[];
  order: number; published: boolean;
};

export async function createService(input: ServiceInput): Promise<Service> {
  return apiFetch<Service>("/api/cms/services", { method: "POST", body: input });
}

export async function updateService(id: string, input: Partial<ServiceInput>): Promise<Service> {
  return apiFetch<Service>(`/api/cms/services/${id}`, { method: "PATCH", body: input });
}

export async function deleteService(id: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean }> {
  return apiFetch<{ ok: boolean }>(`/api/cms/services/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
}

// modules/estate/service.ts
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api-client";

export type PropertyStatus = "For Rent" | "For Sale";

export type Property = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  price: string;
  currency: string;
  status: PropertyStatus;
  location: string;
  type: string;
  style: string | null;
  rooms: number;
  bedrooms: number;
  bathrooms: number;
  parking: number;
  area: number;
  images: string[];
  amenities: string[];
  options: string[];
  published: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
};

export type EstateFilters = {
  search?: string; status?: string; location?: string; type?: string; style?: string;
  minRooms?: number; minBedrooms?: number; minBathrooms?: number; minParking?: number;
  minArea?: number; maxPrice?: number; amenities?: string[]; options?: string[];
};

export async function listProperties(filters: EstateFilters = {}): Promise<Property[]> {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams();
  qs.set("published", "true");
  if (filters.search) qs.set("search", filters.search);
  if (filters.status) qs.set("status", filters.status);
  if (filters.location) qs.set("location", filters.location);
  if (filters.type) qs.set("type", filters.type);
  if (filters.style) qs.set("style", filters.style);
  if (filters.minRooms) qs.set("minRooms", String(filters.minRooms));
  if (filters.minBedrooms) qs.set("minBedrooms", String(filters.minBedrooms));
  if (filters.minBathrooms) qs.set("minBathrooms", String(filters.minBathrooms));
  if (filters.minParking) qs.set("minParking", String(filters.minParking));
  if (filters.minArea) qs.set("minArea", String(filters.minArea));
  if (filters.maxPrice) qs.set("maxPrice", String(filters.maxPrice));
  if (filters.amenities?.length) qs.set("amenities", filters.amenities.join(","));
  if (filters.options?.length) qs.set("options", filters.options.join(","));
  return apiFetch<Property[]>(`/api/estate?${qs.toString()}`);
}

export async function getProperty(id: string): Promise<Property | null> {
  try { return await apiFetch<Property>(`/api/estate/${id}`); }
  catch { return null; }
}

export async function listAllProperties(): Promise<Property[]> {
  return apiFetch<Property[]>("/api/estate/admin/all");
}

export async function getPropertyById(id: string): Promise<Property> {
  return apiFetch<Property>(`/api/estate/admin/${id}`);
}

export type PropertyInput = {
  title: string; description: string; price: number; currency: string;
  status: PropertyStatus; location: string; type: string; style: string | null;
  rooms: number; bedrooms: number; bathrooms: number; parking: number; area: number;
  images: string[]; amenities: string[]; options: string[]; published: boolean;
};

export async function createProperty(input: PropertyInput): Promise<Property> {
  return apiFetch<Property>("/api/estate", { method: "POST", body: input });
}

export async function updateProperty(id: string, input: Partial<PropertyInput>): Promise<Property> {
  return apiFetch<Property>(`/api/estate/${id}`, { method: "PATCH", body: input });
}

export async function deleteProperty(id: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean }> {
  return apiFetch<{ ok: boolean }>(`/api/estate/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
}

// modules/shop/orders/service.ts
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api-client";
import type { CartItem } from "@/modules/cart/service";

export type OrderStatus = "PENDING" | "PAID" | "FULFILLED" | "CANCELLED" | "REFUNDED";

export type OrderItem = {
  id: string;
  productId: string | null;
  title: string;
  price: string;
  quantity: number;
  image: string | null;
};

export type Order = {
  id: string;
  userId: string | null;
  email: string;
  status: OrderStatus;
  currency: string;
  subtotal: string;
  total: string;
  paymentStatus: string;
  cardLast4: string | null;
  cardBrand: string | null;
  shippingName: string | null;
  shippingAddress: string | null;
  shippingCity: string | null;
  shippingRegion: string | null;
  shippingCountry: string | null;
  shippingNotes: string | null;
  items: OrderItem[];
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  user?: { id: string; name: string | null; email: string } | null;
};

export type CheckoutResponse = {
  orderId: string;
  checkoutUrl: string;
  sessionId: string;
};

export async function createCheckout(
  items: CartItem[],
  shipping: {
    name: string; email: string; phone?: string; address: string;
    city: string; region?: string; country: string;
    postalCode?: string; notes?: string;
  },
): Promise<CheckoutResponse> {
  return apiFetch<CheckoutResponse>("/api/checkout", {
    method: "POST",
    body: {
      items: items.map((i) => ({ productId: i.productId, quantity: i.quantity })),
      shipping,
    },
  });
}

export async function getOrderBySession(sessionId: string): Promise<Order> {
  return apiFetch<Order>(`/api/orders/by-session/${sessionId}`);
}

export async function listMyOrders(): Promise<Order[]> {
  return apiFetch<Order[]>("/api/orders/mine");
}

export async function getMyOrder(id: string): Promise<Order> {
  return apiFetch<Order>(`/api/orders/${id}`);
}

export async function listAllOrders(): Promise<Order[]> {
  return apiFetch<Order[]>("/api/orders/admin/all");
}

export async function updateOrder(id: string, input: { status?: OrderStatus }): Promise<Order> {
  return apiFetch<Order>(`/api/orders/${id}`, { method: "PATCH", body: input });
}

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

type Variant =
  | "pending"
  | "paid"
  | "fulfilled"
  | "cancelled"
  | "refunded"
  | "new"
  | "contacted"
  | "converted"
  | "closed"
  | "planning"
  | "in-progress"
  | "on-hold"
  | "completed"
  | "handled"
  | "unhandled"
  | "published"
  | "draft";

const labels: Record<Variant, string> = {
  pending: "Pending",
  paid: "Paid",
  fulfilled: "Fulfilled",
  cancelled: "Cancelled",
  refunded: "Refunded",
  new: "New",
  contacted: "Contacted",
  converted: "Converted",
  closed: "Closed",
  planning: "Planning",
  "in-progress": "In progress",
  "on-hold": "On hold",
  completed: "Completed",
  handled: "Handled",
  unhandled: "Unhandled",
  published: "Published",
  draft: "Draft",
};

export function StatusBadge({ variant }: { variant: Variant }): ReactNode {
  return <span className={`status-badge status-${variant}`}>{labels[variant]}</span>;
}

// Helper mappers
export function orderVariant(s: string): Variant {
  const k = s.toLowerCase() as Variant;
  return ["pending", "paid", "fulfilled", "cancelled", "refunded"].includes(k)
    ? k
    : "pending";
}
export function quoteVariant(s: string): Variant {
  const k = s.toLowerCase() as Variant;
  return ["new", "contacted", "converted", "closed"].includes(k) ? k : "new";
}
export function projectVariant(s: string): Variant {
  const k = s.toLowerCase().replace("_", "-") as Variant;
  return ["planning", "in-progress", "on-hold", "completed"].includes(k) ? k : "planning";
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

type Props = {
  id: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  autoComplete?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  minLength?: number;
  disabled?: boolean;
  placeholder?: string;
};

export function PasswordInput({
  id,
  value,
  onChange,
  autoComplete = "current-password",
  required,
  minLength,
  disabled,
  placeholder,
}: Props) {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="password-field">
      <input
        id={id}
        type={visible ? "text" : "password"}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        autoComplete={autoComplete}
        required={required}
        minLength={minLength}
        disabled={disabled}
        placeholder={placeholder}
      />
      <button
        type="button"
        className="password-toggle"
        onClick={() => setVisible((v) => !v)}
        aria-label={visible ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}
        tabIndex={-1}
      >
        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">
          {visible ? "visibility_off" : "visibility"}
        </span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: components\dashboard\Shell.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { logout } from "@/modules/auth/session";
import { useSession } from "@/modules/auth/useSession";
import { DashboardSidebar } from "./Sidebar";
import "@/styles/dashboard.css";

export function DashboardShell({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const { user, loading } = useSession();
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();

  // Redirect unauthenticated users once the session has finished loading
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !user) {
      router.replace("/login");
    }
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  // While loading, or while redirect is in flight, show a neutral placeholder
  if (loading || !user) {
    return (
      <div
        style={{
          padding: 40,
          textAlign: "center",
          color: "var(--color-gray)",
        }}
      >
        Loading…
      </div>
    );
  }

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logout();
    router.push("/login");
    router.refresh();
  };

  return (
    <div className="dashboard-shell">
      <DashboardSidebar />
      <div className="dashboard-main">
        <header className="dashboard-topbar">
          <div>
            <p className="topbar-crumb">Dashboard</p>
            <p className="topbar-path">{pathname}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="topbar-user">
            <div>
              <strong>{user.name ?? user.email}</strong>
              <p>{user.email}</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={handleLogout} className="logout-btn" type="button">
              Log out
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>
        <div className="dashboard-content">{children}</div>
        <footer className="dashboard-foot">
          <Link href="/">← Back to website</Link>
        </footer>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { logout } from "@/modules/auth/session";
import { useSession } from "@/modules/auth/useSession";
import "@/styles/header.css";

export function Header() {
  const { user, loading } = useSession();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Close dropdown on outside click + Escape
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onDoc = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDoc);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDoc);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    };
  }, [open]);

  const onLogout = async () => {
    setOpen(false);
    await logout();
    router.push("/");
    router.refresh();
  };

  const displayName = user?.name ?? user?.email ?? "";
  const initial = displayName ? displayName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() : "U";
  const dashboardHref = user?.role === "ADMIN" ? "/admin" : "/dashboard";
  const dashboardLabel = user?.role === "ADMIN" ? "Admin panel" : "Dashboard";

  return (
    <header className="header">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="link-container">
          <p>
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">mail</span>
            <a href="mailto:info@ted-nomex.com">info@ted-nomex.com</a>
          </p>
          <p>
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">location_on</span>
            <a
              href="https://maps.google.com/?q=GJ-275-7823"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
            >
              GJ-275-7823
            </a>
          </p>
          <p>
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">call</span>
            <a href="tel:0595808051">0595808051</a>
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="Contact">
          <p>
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">mail</span>
            <Link href="/contact">our contacts</Link>
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="link-container">
          <div className="signIn-popUp">
            {loading ? (
              <span className="signin-loading" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : user ? (
              <div
                className={`account-trigger ${open ? "is-open" : ""}`}
                ref={menuRef}
              >
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="avatar-btn"
                  onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
                  aria-label="Account menu"
                  aria-haspopup="menu"
                  aria-expanded={open}
                >
                  <span className="avatar-circle">
                    {user.image ? (
                      // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                      <img
                        src={user.image}
                        alt={user.name ?? user.email}
                        style={{
                          width: "100%",
                          height: "100%",
                          borderRadius: "50%",
                          objectFit: "cover",
                          display: "block",
                        }}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      initial
                    )}
                  </span>
                </button>

                <div className="dropdown" role="menu">
                  <div className="dropdown-head">
                    <div>
                      <p className="dropdown-name">
                        {user.name ?? user.email}
                      </p>
                      <p className="dropdown-email">{user.email}</p>
                    </div>
                    {user.role === "ADMIN" && (
                      <span className="admin-badge">Admin</span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <ul>
                    <li>
                      <Link
                        href={dashboardHref}
                        onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                        role="menuitem"
                      >
                        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">
                          dashboard
                        </span>
                        {dashboardLabel}
                      </Link>
                    </li>
                    <li>
                      <Link
                        href="/"
                        onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                        role="menuitem"
                      >
                        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">
                          public
                        </span>
                        Back to website
                      </Link>
                    </li>
                  </ul>

                  <div className="dropdown-foot">
                    <ul>
                      <li>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={onLogout}
                          role="menuitem"
                        >
                          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">
                            logout
                          </span>
                          Log out
                        </button>
                      </li>
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <Link href="/login" className="popUp">
                <span className="material-symbols-outlined">login</span>
                <p>SIGN IN</p>
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="social-media">
            <a href="#" aria-label="Facebook">
              <i className="fab fa-facebook" />
            </a>
            <a href="#" aria-label="Twitter">
              <i className="fab fa-twitter" />
            </a>
            <a href="#" aria-label="Instagram">
              <i className="fab fa-instagram" />
            </a>
            <a href="#" aria-label="YouTube">
              <i className="fab fa-youtube" />
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { getCart, getCartCount, subscribeToCart, type CartItem } from "@/modules/cart/service";
import "@/styles/nav.css";

const NAV_LINKS = [
  { href: "/", label: "home" },
  { href: "/about", label: "about" },
  { href: "/services", label: "services" },
  { href: "/estate", label: "estate" },
  { href: "/news", label: "news" },
  { href: "/projects", label: "project" },
  { href: "/contact", label: "contact" },
];

// Public searchable pages ONLY (no dashboard, no admin).
const SEARCH_INDEX: { href: string; label: string; icon: string; keywords: string }[] = [
  { href: "/", label: "Home", icon: "home", keywords: "home landing main start" },
  { href: "/about", label: "About", icon: "info", keywords: "about story team who we are mission values" },
  { href: "/services", label: "Services", icon: "handyman", keywords: "services what we do construction renovation engineering" },
  { href: "/estate", label: "Estate", icon: "home_work", keywords: "estate properties real estate houses land rent sale apartment" },
  { href: "/news", label: "News", icon: "newspaper", keywords: "news articles blog updates press releases" },
  { href: "/projects", label: "Projects", icon: "apartment", keywords: "projects portfolio completed work case studies" },
  { href: "/product", label: "Products", icon: "inventory_2", keywords: "products shop store buy materials tools supplies" },
  { href: "/cart", label: "Cart", icon: "shopping_cart", keywords: "cart basket items shopping" },
  { href: "/checkout", label: "Checkout", icon: "payment", keywords: "checkout payment pay order complete stripe" },
  { href: "/cost", label: "Cost Calculator", icon: "calculate", keywords: "calculator cost estimate quote price budget" },
  { href: "/contact", label: "Contact", icon: "mail", keywords: "contact message email call phone get in touch" },
  { href: "/login", label: "Sign in", icon: "person", keywords: "login sign in account access" },
  { href: "/register", label: "Create account", icon: "person_add", keywords: "register sign up create account new user" },
  { href: "/forgot-password", label: "Forgot password", icon: "lock_reset", keywords: "forgot reset password recovery" },
];

function searchPages(query: string): typeof SEARCH_INDEX {
  const q = query.toLowerCase().trim();
  if (!q) return SEARCH_INDEX;
  return SEARCH_INDEX.filter((entry) => {
    const hay = `${entry.label} ${entry.keywords} ${entry.href}`.toLowerCase();
    return hay.includes(q);
  });
}
export function Nav() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [shareUrl, setShareUrl] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => { setShareUrl(window.location.href); }, []);
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false);
  const [openMenu, setOpenMenu] = useState<"mobile" | "cart" | "share" | "search" | null>(null);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
  const [cartItems, setCartItems] = useState<CartItem[]>([]);
  const [cartCount, setCartCount] = useState(0);
  const [cartSubtotal, setCartSubtotal] = useState(0);
  const [badgePop, setBadgePop] = useState(false);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const searchInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const searchResults = useMemo(() => searchPages(searchTerm), [searchTerm]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 20);
    onScroll();
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!openMenu) return;
    const onDoc = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpenMenu(null);
    };
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === "Escape") setOpenMenu(null); };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDoc);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDoc);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    };
  }, [openMenu]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const refreshCart = () => {
      const items = getCart();
      setCartItems(items);
      setCartCount(getCartCount());
      setCartSubtotal(items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + Number(i.price) * i.quantity, 0));
    };
    refreshCart();
    const onVis = () => { if (!document.hidden) refreshCart(); };
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
    const unsubscribe = subscribeToCart(refreshCart);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
      unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (cartCount === 0) return;
    setBadgePop(true);
    const t = setTimeout(() => setBadgePop(false), 320);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [cartCount]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setActiveIndex(0);
  }, [searchTerm]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (openMenu === "search") {
      setTimeout(() => searchInputRef.current?.focus(), 50);
    }
  }, [openMenu]);

  const toggle = (k: NonNullable<typeof openMenu>) => setOpenMenu((p) => (p === k ? null : k));
  const isActive = (href: string) => href === "/" ? pathname === "/" : pathname.startsWith(href);

  const copyLink = async () => {
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl); setOpenMenu(null); } catch {}
  };

  const goToResult = (href: string) => {
    setOpenMenu(null);
    setSearchTerm("");
    router.push(href);
  };

  const onSearchKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown") {
      e.preventDefault();
      setActiveIndex((i) => Math.min(i + 1, searchResults.length - 1));
    } else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") {
      e.preventDefault();
      setActiveIndex((i) => Math.max(i - 1, 0));
    } else if (e.key === "Enter") {
      e.preventDefault();
      const target = searchResults[activeIndex];
      if (target) goToResult(target.href);
    }
  };

  const shareUrls = {
    facebook: `https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=${encodeURIComponent(shareUrl)}`,
    twitter: `https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=${encodeURIComponent(shareUrl)}`,
  };

  return (
    <nav className={`nav${scrolled ? " scrolled" : ""}`}>
      <div className="container">
        <div className="logo-container"><Link href="/">Ted-Nomex</Link></div>
        <div className="links-container" ref={menuRef}>
          <ul className="items-container">
            {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
              <li key={link.href} className="items">
                <Link href={link.href} className={isActive(link.href) ? "active" : undefined}>
                  <p>{link.label}</p>
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>

          <div className="icons-container">
            <div className="icon" id="DropDow" onClick={() => toggle("mobile")} role="button" tabIndex={0} aria-label="Open menu">
              <div className="item"><span className="material-symbols-outlined">{openMenu === "mobile" ? "close" : "menu"}</span></div>
            </div>

            <div className="icon" onClick={() => toggle("cart")} role="button" tabIndex={0} aria-label="Cart">
              <div className="item">
                <span className="material-symbols-outlined">local_mall</span>
                <div className={`value${badgePop ? " pop" : ""}`}>{cartCount}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="icon" onClick={() => toggle("share")} role="button" tabIndex={0} aria-label="Share">
              <div className="item"><span className="material-symbols-outlined">share</span></div>
            </div>

            <div className="icon" onClick={() => toggle("search")} role="button" tabIndex={0} aria-label="Search">
              <div className="item"><span className="material-symbols-outlined">search</span></div>
            </div>

            <div className={`dropdown-panel mobile-menu-panel${openMenu === "mobile" ? " is-open" : ""}`}>
              {NAV_LINKS.map((l) => (
                <Link key={l.href} href={l.href} className={isActive(l.href) ? "active" : undefined} onClick={() => setOpenMenu(null)}>{l.label}</Link>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className={`dropdown-panel cart-panel${openMenu === "cart" ? " is-open" : ""}`}>
              <div className="head"><p>Your Cart</p><p><span>{cartCount}</span> items</p></div>
              {cartCount === 0 ? <p className="empty">Your cart is empty</p> : null}
              <div className="total">
                <p>Subtotal:</p>
                <p>{cartItems.length === 0 ? "$0" : `${cartItems[0]?.currency ?? "GHS"} ${cartSubtotal.toLocaleString()}`}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="link-items">
                <Link href="/cart" className="btn" onClick={() => setOpenMenu(null)}>view cart</Link>
                <Link href="/checkout" className="btn" onClick={() => setOpenMenu(null)}>checkout</Link>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className={`dropdown-panel share-panel${openMenu === "share" ? " is-open" : ""}`}>
              <a href={shareUrls.facebook} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><i className="fab fa-facebook" /> Facebook</a>
              <a href={shareUrls.twitter} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><i className="fab fa-twitter" /> Twitter</a>
              <a href="https://www.instagram.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><i className="fab fa-instagram" /> Instagram</a>
              <a href="https://www.youtube.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><i className="fab fa-youtube" /> YouTube</a>
              <button type="button" onClick={copyLink} style={{ gridColumn: "1 / -1" }}><span className="material-symbols-outlined">link</span> Copy link</button>
            </div>

            <div className={`dropdown-panel search-panel${openMenu === "search" ? " is-open" : ""}`}>
              <div className="search-container">
                <input
                  ref={searchInputRef}
                  type="text"
                  className="searchInput"
                  placeholder="Search pages… (try 'services' or 'contact')"
                  value={searchTerm}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={onSearchKeyDown}
                  aria-label="Search pages"
                  autoComplete="off"
                />
                <button
                  className="search-button"
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    const target = searchResults[activeIndex];
                    if (target) goToResult(target.href);
                  }}
                  aria-label="Go to first result"
                >
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              <ul className="results" role="listbox">
                {searchResults.length === 0 ? (
                  <li className="no-results">
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined">search_off</span>
                    No pages match “{searchTerm}”
                  </li>
                ) : (
                  searchResults.map((r, i) => (
                    <li
                      key={r.href}
                      role="option"
                      aria-selected={i === activeIndex}
                      className={i === activeIndex ? "active" : undefined}
                      onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIndex(i)}
                      onClick={() => goToResult(r.href)}
                    >
                      <span className="material-symbols-outlined">{r.icon}</span>
                      <div className="result-text">
                        <p className="result-label">{r.label}</p>
                        <p className="result-path">{r.href}</p>
                      </div>
                      <span className="material-symbols-outlined result-arrow">arrow_forward</span>
                    </li>
                  ))
                )}
              </ul>
            </div>
          </div>

          <Link href="/cost" className="calculator-container">
            <p>Cost Calculator</p>
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">function</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

"use client";

export function ScrollToTop() {
  const onClick = () => {
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
  };

  return (
    <button type="button" onClick={onClick} aria-label="Back to top">
      <span className="material-symbols-outlined">expand_less</span>
    </button>
  );
}

FILE: components\marketing\Counter.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

function useCounter(target: number, duration = 1800) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(0);
  const started = useRef(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
      if (entry.isIntersecting && !started.current) {
        started.current = true;
        const start = performance.now();
        const tick = (now: number) => {
          const progress = Math.min((now - start) / duration, 1);
          setValue(Math.round(progress * target));
          if (progress < 1) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        };
        requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
    }, { threshold: 0.3 });
    io.observe(el);
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, [target, duration]);

  return { value, ref };
}

function CounterItem({ label, target, icon }: { label: string; target: number; icon?: string }) {
  const { value, ref } = useCounter(target);
  return (
    <div className="list-item" ref={ref}>
      {icon && <span className="material-symbols-outlined">{icon}</span>}
      <div className="list">
        <div className="counter">{value}</div>
        <p>{label}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function Counters({ data, style }: { data: { label: string; target: number }[]; style?: "static" | "plain" }) {
  if (style === "plain") {
    return (
      <div className="list-items">
        {data.map((c) => (
          <div key={c.label} className="list">
            <CounterItemPlain label={c.label} target={c.target} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="counter-container">
      {data.map((c) => (
        <CounterItem key={c.label} label={c.label} target={c.target} icon="add" />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function CounterItemPlain({ label, target }: { label: string; target: number }) {
  const { value, ref } = useCounter(target);
  return (
    <div ref={ref}>
      <div className="counter">{value}</div>
      <p>{label}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { IMAGES } from "@/lib/assets";

const SLIDES = [
  {
    image: IMAGES.homeSlides[0],
    kicker: "guaranteed high quality",
    title: "Welcome to the Ted-Nomex",
    highlight: "construction services",
    extra: "company",
    description: "Welcome to Ted-Nomex, your trusted partner in construction services. We are committed to delivering high-quality results for your projects.",
    cta: { label: "View our service", href: "/services" },
    stat: { value: "12+", label: "Years of Craft" },
  },
  {
    image: IMAGES.homeSlides[1],
    kicker: "Architecture Project",
    title: "Our New Project",
    highlight: "World trade center",
    extra: "",
    description: "Discover our latest architectural masterpiece, the World Trade Center. We blend innovation and elegance to create iconic structures.",
    cta: { label: "View project details", href: "/projects" },
    stat: { value: "461", label: "Projects Delivered" },
  },
  {
    image: IMAGES.homeSlides[2],
    kicker: "guaranteed high quality",
    title: "I need an equipment on rent?",
    highlight: "Start today?",
    extra: "",
    description: "Embark on your construction journey with ease. Rent top-quality equipment from us and get your project started today.",
    cta: { label: "View our Shop", href: "/product" },
    stat: { value: "55+", label: "Machines Ready" },
  },
];

export function HeroSlider() {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => setIndex((i) => (i + 1) % SLIDES.length), 9000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  const prev = () => setIndex((i) => (i - 1 + SLIDES.length) % SLIDES.length);
  const next = () => setIndex((i) => (i + 1) % SLIDES.length);
  const active = SLIDES[index];

  return (
    <section className="hero-home" id="home">
      {/* Layered backgrounds */}
      <div className="hero-bg-stack">
        {SLIDES.map((slide, i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            className={`hero-bg${i === index ? " active" : ""}`}
            style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${slide.image}')` }}
          />
        ))}
        <div className="hero-bg-overlay" />
        <div className="hero-grain" />
      </div>

      {/* Decorative floating shapes */}
      <div className="hero-shape hero-shape-1" />
      <div className="hero-shape hero-shape-2" />
      <div className="hero-shape hero-shape-3" />

      {/* Main content */}
      <div className="hero-content container">
        <div className="hero-grid">
          {/* Left column: text */}
          <div className="hero-text" key={index}>
            <div className="hero-kicker">
              <span className="hero-kicker-dot" />
              <span>{active.kicker}</span>
            </div>

            <h1 className="hero-headline">
              <span className="hero-line">{active.title}</span>
              <span className="hero-line hero-line-accent">{active.highlight}</span>
              {active.extra && <span className="hero-line">{active.extra}</span>}
            </h1>

            <p className="hero-lead">{active.description}</p>

            <div className="hero-cta-row">
              <Link href={active.cta.href} className="hero-cta-primary">
                <span>{active.cta.label}</span>
                <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/contact" className="hero-cta-ghost">
                Get in touch
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right column: stat + progress */}
          <div className="hero-side">
            <div className="hero-stat" key={`stat-${index}`}>
              <div className="hero-stat-value">{active.stat.value}</div>
              <div className="hero-stat-label">{active.stat.label}</div>
            </div>

            <div className="hero-progress">
              {SLIDES.map((_, i) => (
                <button
                  key={i}
                  className={`hero-progress-item${i === index ? " active" : ""}`}
                  onClick={() => setIndex(i)}
                  aria-label={`Go to slide ${i + 1}`}
                >
                  <span className="hero-progress-bar" />
                  <span className="hero-progress-num">0{i + 1}</span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Bottom bar: controls + scroll hint */}
      <div className="hero-bottom container">
        <div className="hero-controls">
          <button type="button" onClick={prev} aria-label="Previous slide" className="hero-arrow">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_back</span>
          </button>
          <div className="hero-counter">
            <span className="hero-counter-current">0{index + 1}</span>
            <span className="hero-counter-divider">/</span>
            <span className="hero-counter-total">0{SLIDES.length}</span>
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={next} aria-label="Next slide" className="hero-arrow">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <a href="#services" className="hero-scroll">
          <span className="hero-scroll-line" />
          <span>Scroll to explore</span>
        </a>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { listServicesCached } from "@/modules/cms/services/cached";

const SLUG_ICONS: Record<string, string> = {
  "design-and-build": "architecture",
  "design-build": "architecture",
  "tiling-and-painting": "format_paint",
  "architecture": "apartment",
  "house-renovation": "home_repair_service",
  "interior-renovation": "home_repair_service",
  "household-repairs": "handyman",
  "preconstruction-planning": "engineering",
  "interior-design": "chair",
  "equipment-rental": "construction",
};

function iconForSlug(slug: string): string {
  return SLUG_ICONS[slug] ?? "handyman";
}

export async function ServicesGrid() {
  const all = await listServicesCached();
  const services = all.slice(0, 3);

  if (services.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="services-items services-items-empty">
        <div className="services-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">handyman</span>
          <p>Our service listings are being prepared. Check back soon.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="services-items">
      {services.map((service, i) => (
        <div key={service.id} className="service-list">
          <div
            className="content-container"
            style={{
              background: service.coverImage
                ? `url('${service.coverImage}') no-repeat center center / cover`
                : undefined,
            }}
          >
            <div className="content">
              <Link href={`/services/${service.slug}`} className="btn">
                Read more
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="collection">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">
              {iconForSlug(service.slug)}
            </span>
            <br />
            <br />
            <h2>{service.title}</h2>
            <p>{service.excerpt}</p>
            <div className="line-container">
              <span className="one"></span>
              <span className="two"></span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="num">
            <p>{String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import type { Product } from "@/modules/shop/products/service";
import { useCart } from "@/lib/cart";

function currencySymbol(code?: string | null) {
  const c = (code ?? "GHS").toUpperCase();
  if (c === "GHS") return "GHâ‚µ";
  if (c === "USD") return "$";
  if (c === "EUR") return "â‚¬";
  if (c === "GBP") return "Â£";
  return c;
}

export function ProductCard({ product }: { product: Product }) {
  const { addToCart } = useCart();
  const cover = product.images?.[0] ?? "";
  const href = `/product/${product.slug}`;
  const currency = currencySymbol(product.currency);
  const outOfStock = product.stock <= 0;

  return (
    <Link href={href} className="product-card">
      <div
        className={cover ? "prc-image" : "prc-image prc-image-empty"}
        style={cover ? { backgroundImage: `url('${cover}')` } : undefined}
      >
        {product.category && (
          <span className="prc-cat">{product.category}</span>
        )}
        {outOfStock && (
          <span className="prc-stock prc-stock-out">Out of stock</span>
        )}
        <span className="prc-price-tag">
          <span className="prc-price-currency">{currency}</span>
          {Number(product.price).toLocaleString()}
        </span>
        {!outOfStock && (
          <button
            type="button"
            className="prc-quick-add"
            aria-label={`Add ${product.name} to cart`}
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              e.stopPropagation();
              addToCart(product, 1);
            }}
          >
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">add_shopping_cart</span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="prc-body">
        <h3>{product.name}</h3>
        {product.description && (
          <p className="prc-desc">{product.description}</p>
        )}
        <span className="prc-link">
          View product
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
        </span>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import type { Product } from "@/modules/shop/products/service";
import { useCart } from "@/lib/cart";

type DetailProps = {
  product: Product;
  related?: Product[];
};

function currencySymbol(code?: string | null) {
  const c = (code ?? "GHS").toUpperCase();
  if (c === "GHS") return "GHâ‚µ";
  if (c === "USD") return "$";
  if (c === "EUR") return "â‚¬";
  if (c === "GBP") return "Â£";
  return c;
}

export function ProductDetail({ product, related = [] }: DetailProps) {
  const { addToCart } = useCart();
  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);
  const [added, setAdded] = useState(false);

  const images = product.images ?? [];
  const cover = images[0] ?? "";
  const gallery = images.slice(1);
  const currency = currencySymbol(product.currency);
  const outOfStock = product.stock <= 0;
  const lowStock = !outOfStock && product.stock <= 5;

  const handleAdd = () => {
    if (outOfStock) return;
    addToCart(product, qty);
    setAdded(true);
    setTimeout(() => setAdded(false), 1800);
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* â”€â”€ Hero â”€â”€ */}
      <section className="product-detail-hero">
        <div
          className="product-detail-hero-bg"
          style={cover ? { backgroundImage: `url('${cover}')` } : undefined}
        />
        <div className="product-detail-hero-overlay" />
        <div className="container">
          <nav className="hero-chip" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
            <Link href="/">Home</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <Link href="/product">Shop</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <span className="current">{product.name}</span>
          </nav>

          <div className="product-detail-hero-content">
            <div className="product-detail-hero-meta">
              {product.category && (
                <span className="product-detail-status">{product.category}</span>
              )}
              {outOfStock ? (
                <span className="product-detail-type product-detail-type-warn">
                  Out of stock
                </span>
              ) : lowStock ? (
                <span className="product-detail-type product-detail-type-warn">
                  Only {product.stock} left
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span className="product-detail-type">
                  In stock Â· {product.stock}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <h1 className="product-detail-title">{product.name}</h1>
            <div className="product-detail-price">
              <span className="product-detail-price-currency">{currency}</span>
              <span className="product-detail-price-value">
                {Number(product.price).toLocaleString()}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* â”€â”€ Body â”€â”€ */}
      <section className="product-detail-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="product-detail-layout">
            <div className="product-detail-main">
              {product.description && (
                <div className="product-detail-section">
                  <p className="product-detail-kicker">About this product</p>
                  <h2>Description</h2>
                  <p className="product-detail-desc">{product.description}</p>
                </div>
              )}

              {gallery.length > 0 && (
                <div className="product-detail-section">
                  <p className="product-detail-kicker">Gallery</p>
                  <h2>More images</h2>
                  <div className="product-gallery">
                    {gallery.map((src, i) => (
                      <div
                        key={`${src}-${i}`}
                        className="product-gallery-item"
                        style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${src}')` }}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="product-detail-section">
                <p className="product-detail-kicker">Overview</p>
                <h2>Key details</h2>
                <div className="product-detail-facts">
                  {product.category && (
                    <div className="fact">
                      <span className="fact-label">Category</span>
                      <span className="fact-value">{product.category}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="fact">
                    <span className="fact-label">Price</span>
                    <span className="fact-value">
                      {currency} {Number(product.price).toLocaleString()}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="fact">
                    <span className="fact-label">Availability</span>
                    <span className="fact-value">
                      {outOfStock ? "Out of stock" : `${product.stock} in stock`}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="fact">
                    <span className="fact-label">SKU</span>
                    <span className="fact-value">{product.slug}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <aside className="product-detail-sidebar">
              <div className="product-detail-card">
                <p className="product-detail-card-kicker">Price</p>
                <div className="product-detail-card-price">
                  <span>{currency}</span>
                  {Number(product.price).toLocaleString()}
                </div>

                <div className="product-qty">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                    onClick={() => setQty((q) => Math.max(1, q - 1))}
                    disabled={qty <= 1}
                  >
                    âˆ’
                  </button>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={1}
                    max={Math.max(1, product.stock)}
                    value={qty}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const n = Number(e.target.value) || 1;
                      setQty(Math.min(Math.max(1, product.stock || 1), Math.max(1, n)));
                    }}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label="Increase quantity"
                    onClick={() => setQty((q) => Math.min(Math.max(1, product.stock), q + 1))}
                    disabled={qty >= product.stock}
                  >
                    +
                  </button>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="product-detail-card-cta"
                  onClick={handleAdd}
                  disabled={outOfStock}
                >
                  {added ? "Added to cart âœ“" : outOfStock ? "Out of stock" : "Add to cart"}
                  {!outOfStock && !added && (
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined">shopping_cart</span>
                  )}
                </button>

                <Link href="/cart" className="product-detail-card-cta-ghost">
                  View cart
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                </Link>

                {lowStock && (
                  <p className="product-detail-card-note">
                    Only {product.stock} left â€” order soon.
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="product-detail-agent">
                <div className="product-detail-agent-avatar">T</div>
                <div>
                  <p className="product-detail-agent-name">Ted-Nomex Supplies</p>
                  <p className="product-detail-agent-role">Ships across Ghana</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </aside>
          </div>

          {related.length > 0 && (
            <div className="product-related">
              <div className="product-related-head">
                <h2>You may also need</h2>
                <Link href="/product" className="product-related-link">
                  View all products
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                </Link>
              </div>
              <div className="product-grid">
                {related.map((p) => (
                  <ProductRelatedCard key={p.id} product={p} />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

function ProductRelatedCard({ product }: { product: Product }) {
  const cover = product.images?.[0] ?? "";
  const currency = currencySymbol(product.currency);
  return (
    <Link href={`/product/${product.slug}`} className="product-card">
      <div
        className={cover ? "prc-image" : "prc-image prc-image-empty"}
        style={cover ? { backgroundImage: `url('${cover}')` } : undefined}
      >
        {product.category && <span className="prc-cat">{product.category}</span>}
        <span className="prc-price-tag">
          <span className="prc-price-currency">{currency}</span>
          {Number(product.price).toLocaleString()}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="prc-body">
        <h3>{product.name}</h3>
        {product.description && <p className="prc-desc">{product.description}</p>}
        <span className="prc-link">
          View product
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
        </span>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: components\marketing\TeamSlider.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import "@/styles/team-slider.css";

export type TeamMember = {
  name: string;
  role: string;
  img: string;
  desc: string;
};

type Props = { team: TeamMember[] };

// Extended bios â€” keyed by name so the slider data can stay as-is.
// Extend these as you add people. Falls back to `member.desc` if missing.
const BIOS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  "Nodin Somuah": [
    "Nodin Somuah founded Ted-Nomex in 2012 with a simple conviction: construction in Ghana deserved better â€” better planning, better craft, better communication with clients. Twelve years later, that conviction is still the operating principle.",
    "As Founder and CEO, Nodin oversees the firm's strategic direction, capital planning, and the client relationships that define the company's reputation. He is directly involved in every major project from pitch to handover â€” no project goes out the door without his review.",
    "Nodin holds a BSc in Civil Engineering and has spent his career split between site work and firm leadership. He sits on the board of two industry associations and mentors young engineers entering the field.",
  ],
  "Jeffrey Luke": [
    "Jeffrey Luke runs the operational machinery behind Ted-Nomex: scheduling, procurement logistics, permit coordination, and the daily rhythm that keeps multiple concurrent sites moving.",
    "Before joining Ted-Nomex he spent eight years in logistics for a regional construction supplier, where he learned the same lesson on the supply side: projects don't fail from bad drawings, they fail from late material deliveries. He brought that insight to the operations team and it changed how the firm schedules.",
    "Jeffrey manages a small team of coordinators and works closely with the site managers to make sure nothing waits.",
  ],
  "Austin Evon": [
    "Austin Evon is the Main Manager on site â€” the person who turns plans into structures. He supervises the construction teams across all active projects, enforces quality standards, and signs off on every phase transition.",
    "Austin came up through the trades: ten years as a site foreman before stepping into management. That background shows in how he runs jobs â€” he can read a structural drawing as fast as the engineer who produced it, and he knows when a detail is going to be a problem six weeks down the line.",
    "He is certified in construction safety management and runs the firm's internal training program for junior site staff.",
  ],
};

export function TeamSlider({ team }: Props) {
  const trackRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
  const [canScrollLeft, setCanScrollLeft] = useState(false);
  const [canScrollRight, setCanScrollRight] = useState(true);
  const [openIdx, setOpenIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const updateButtons = () => {
    const el = trackRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    setCanScrollLeft(el.scrollLeft > 8);
    setCanScrollRight(el.scrollLeft + el.clientWidth < el.scrollWidth - 8);
    const cardWidth = el.scrollWidth / team.length;
    const idx = Math.round(el.scrollLeft / cardWidth);
    setActive(Math.min(team.length - 1, Math.max(0, idx)));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = trackRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    el.addEventListener("scroll", updateButtons, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("resize", updateButtons);
    updateButtons();
    return () => {
      el.removeEventListener("scroll", updateButtons);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateButtons);
    };
  }, [team.length]);

  // Modal: lock body scroll + Esc
  useEffect(() => {
    if (openIdx === null) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") setOpenIdx(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };
  }, [openIdx]);

  const scrollToIndex = (idx: number) => {
    const el = trackRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const cardWidth = el.scrollWidth / team.length;
    el.scrollTo({ left: cardWidth * idx, behavior: "smooth" });
  };

  const goPrev = () => scrollToIndex(Math.max(0, active - 1));
  const goNext = () => scrollToIndex(Math.min(team.length - 1, active + 1));

  const activeMember = openIdx !== null ? team[openIdx] : null;
  const activeBio =
    activeMember && BIOS[activeMember.name]
      ? BIOS[activeMember.name]
      : activeMember
      ? [activeMember.desc]
      : [];

  return (
    <>
      <div className="team-slider">
        <div className="team-slider-controls">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="team-slider-arrow"
            onClick={goPrev}
            disabled={!canScrollLeft}
            aria-label="Previous team member"
          >
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_back</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="team-slider-arrow"
            onClick={goNext}
            disabled={!canScrollRight}
            aria-label="Next team member"
          >
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="team-slider-track" ref={trackRef}>
          {team.map((t, i) => (
            <article
              className="team-card team-card-clickable"
              key={t.name}
              onClick={() => setOpenIdx(i)}
              role="button"
              tabIndex={0}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  setOpenIdx(i);
                }
              }}
              aria-label={`Read more about ${t.name}`}
            >
              <div
                className="team-card-image"
                style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${t.img}')` }}
              >
                <div className="team-card-overlay">
                  <span className="team-card-cta">
                    <span>Read more</span>
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined">
                      arrow_outward
                    </span>
                  </span>
                </div>
                <span className="team-card-num">
                  {String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="team-card-body">
                <div className="team-card-head">
                  <h3 className="team-card-name">{t.name}</h3>
                  <span className="team-role-pill">{t.role}</span>
                </div>
                <p className="team-card-desc">{t.desc}</p>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="team-slider-dots">
          {team.map((_, i) => (
            <button
              type="button"
              key={i}
              className={`team-slider-dot${i === active ? " active" : ""}`}
              onClick={() => scrollToIndex(i)}
              aria-label={`Go to team member ${i + 1}`}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {activeMember && (
        <div
          className="team-modal-backdrop"
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-labelledby="team-modal-title"
          onClick={() => setOpenIdx(null)}
        >
          <div
            className="team-modal"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              className="team-modal-close"
              onClick={() => setOpenIdx(null)}
              aria-label="Close"
            >
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">close</span>
            </button>

            <div
              className="team-modal-image"
              style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${activeMember.img}')` }}
            >
              <span className="team-modal-num">
                {String((openIdx ?? 0) + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="team-modal-body">
              <span className="team-modal-role">{activeMember.role}</span>
              <h2 id="team-modal-title" className="team-modal-name">
                {activeMember.name}
              </h2>

              <div className="team-modal-bio">
                {activeBio.map((p, i) => (
                  <p key={i}>{p}</p>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="team-modal-foot">
                <p>Want to work with {activeMember.name.split(" ")[0]}?</p>
                <Link
                  href="/contact"
                  className="team-modal-cta"
                  onClick={() => setOpenIdx(null)}
                >
                  Get in touch
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">
                    arrow_forward
                  </span>
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

export function WhyChooseUs() {

      if (e.key === "Escape") setOpenId(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };
  }, [openId]);

  return (

                  onOpen={() => setOpenId(r.id)}

function ReasonCard({
  reason,
  index,
  onOpen,
}: {
  reason: Reason;
  index: number;
  onOpen: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className="item why-card-clickable"
      onClick={onOpen}
      role="button"
      tabIndex={0}
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
          e.preventDefault();
          onOpen();
        }
      }}
      aria-label={`Read more about ${reason.title}`}
    >
      <div className="line"></div>
      <div className="list">
        <span className="material-symbols-outlined icon">{reason.icon}</span>
        <div className="Text">
          <h5>{reason.title}</h5>
          <p>{reason.summary}</p>
          <p className="link">read more</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="num">
        <p>{String(index).padStart(2, "0")}.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { getAdminEnquiries } from "@/lib/admin";

export const metadata = { title: "Admin â€” Enquiries" };

export default async function AdminEnquiriesPage() {
  let enquiries: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getAdminEnquiries>> = [];
  let error: string | null = null;
  try {
    enquiries = await getAdminEnquiries();
  } catch (err) {
    error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not load enquiries";
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div>
          <h1>Enquiries</h1>
          <p>{enquiries.length} total</p>
        </div>
      </header>
