      {enquiries.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">mail</span>
          <h3>No enquiries yet</h3>
          <p>Contact form submissions will appear here.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Name</th>
                <th>Email</th>
                <th>Subject</th>
                <th>Status</th>
                <th>Date</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {enquiries.map((e) => (
                <tr key={e.id}>
                  <td>
                    <strong>{e.name}</strong>
                  </td>
                  <td>{e.email}</td>
                  <td>{e.subject ?? "â€”"}</td>
                  <td>
                    <StatusBadge variant={e.handled ? "handled" : "unhandled"} />
                  </td>
                  <td>{new Date(e.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("en-GB")}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app\(admin)\admin\estate\page.tsx
════════════════════════════════════════════
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import "@/styles/admin.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

type Property = {
  id: string; title: string; description: string; price: string; currency: string;
  status: string; location: string; type: string; style: string | null;
  rooms: number; bedrooms: number; bathrooms: number; parking: number; area: number;
  images: string[]; amenities: string[]; options: string[]; published: boolean;
};

type Draft = {
  title: string; description: string; price: string; currency: string;
  status: string; location: string; type: string; style: string;
  rooms: number; bedrooms: number; bathrooms: number; parking: number; area: number;
  images: string[]; amenitiesText: string; optionsText: string; published: boolean;
};

const emptyDraft: Draft = {
  title: "", description: "", price: "0", currency: "GHS",
  status: "For Sale", location: "", type: "", style: "",
  rooms: 1, bedrooms: 1, bathrooms: 1, parking: 0, area: 0,
  images: [], amenitiesText: "", optionsText: "", published: true,
};

function splitLines(s: string) {
  return s.split(/\r?\n|,/).map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}
function formatPrice(amount: string, currency: string) {
  const symbol = currency === "GHS" ? "GHâ‚µ" : currency;
  return `${symbol} ${Number(amount).toLocaleString()}`;
}

async function apiCall(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown) {
  const res = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, {
    method, credentials: "include",
    headers: body ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : undefined,
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });
  let json: any = null;
  try { json = await res.json(); } catch {}
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json?.message ?? `Failed (${res.status})`);
  return json;
}

async function uploadImage(file: File, folder: string): Promise<string> {
  const signRes = await fetch(`${API}/api/media/sign`, {
    method: "POST", credentials: "include",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ folder }),
  });
  if (!signRes.ok) {
    const e = await signRes.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Sign failed (${signRes.status}): ${e.slice(0, 150)}`);
  }
  const sig: any = await signRes.json();
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append("file", file);
  form.append("api_key", sig.apiKey);
  form.append("timestamp", String(sig.timestamp));
  form.append("signature", sig.signature);
  form.append("folder", sig.folder);
  const up = await fetch(`https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${sig.cloudName}/image/upload`, { method: "POST", body: form });
  if (!up.ok) {
    const t = await up.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Upload failed (${up.status}): ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  return (await up.json()).secure_url;
}

function ImagesField({ values, onChange }: { values: string[]; onChange: (v: string[]) => void }) {
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const onFiles = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const files = Array.from(e.target.files ?? []);
    if (files.length === 0) return;
    setError(null); setUploading(true);
    const next = [...values];
    try {
      for (const file of files) {
        if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) continue;
        if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) continue;
        next.push(await uploadImage(file, "ted-nomex/estate"));
      }
      onChange(next);
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Upload failed"); }
    finally { setUploading(false); if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = ""; }
  };

  return (
    <div className="admin-field admin-field-full">
      <label>Images</label>
      <div className="admin-upload-grid">
        {values.map((url, i) => (
          <div key={`${url}-${i}`} className="admin-upload-thumb">
            {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
            <img src={url} alt={`Image ${i + 1}`} />
            <button type="button" className="admin-upload-thumb-remove" onClick={() => onChange(values.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i))} aria-label="Remove">
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">close</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
        <button type="button" className="admin-upload-add" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} disabled={uploading}>
          {uploading ? (<span className="material-symbols-outlined">progress_activity</span>) : (<><span className="material-symbols-outlined">add_photo_alternate</span>Add</>)}
        </button>
      </div>
      <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept="image/*" multiple style={{ display: "none" }} onChange={onFiles} />
      <p className="admin-field-hint">JPG, PNG or WebP, up to 8MB each. Upload multiple at once.</p>
      {error && <p className="admin-field-error">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AdminEstatePage() {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Property[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Property | null>(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Draft>(emptyDraft);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = async () => {
    setLoading(true); setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/estate/admin/all`, { credentials: "include" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed (${res.status})`);
      setRows(await res.json());
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load"); }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  };

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  const openCreate = () => { setEditing(null); setDraft(emptyDraft); setSaveError(null); setOpen(true); };
  const openEdit = (row: Property) => {
    setEditing(row);
    setDraft({
      title: row.title, description: row.description, price: String(row.price),
      currency: row.currency, status: row.status, location: row.location,
      type: row.type, style: row.style ?? "", rooms: row.rooms,
      bedrooms: row.bedrooms, bathrooms: row.bathrooms, parking: row.parking,
      area: row.area, images: row.images ?? [],
      amenitiesText: (row.amenities ?? []).join("\n"),
      optionsText: (row.options ?? []).join("\n"),
      published: row.published,
    });
    setSaveError(null); setOpen(true);
  };
  const closeDrawer = () => { if (saving) return; setOpen(false); setEditing(null); };
  const set = <K extends keyof Draft>(k: K, v: Draft[K]) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, [k]: v }));

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setSaving(true); setSaveError(null);
    try {
      const payload = {
        title: draft.title, description: draft.description,
        price: Number(draft.price), currency: draft.currency,
        status: draft.status, location: draft.location, type: draft.type,
        style: draft.style || null,
        rooms: Number(draft.rooms), bedrooms: Number(draft.bedrooms),
        bathrooms: Number(draft.bathrooms), parking: Number(draft.parking),
        area: Number(draft.area), images: draft.images,
        amenities: splitLines(draft.amenitiesText),
        options: splitLines(draft.optionsText),
        published: draft.published,
      };
      if (editing) await apiCall("PATCH", `/api/estate/${editing.id}`, payload);
      else await apiCall("POST", "/api/estate", payload);
      setOpen(false); setEditing(null); await load();
    } catch (err) { setSaveError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save"); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  const del = async (row: Property) => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete "${row.title}"?`)) return;
    try { await apiCall("DELETE", `/api/estate/${row.id}`); await load(); }
    catch (err) { alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to delete"); }
  };

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div><h1>Estate</h1><p>{rows.length} total</p></div>
        <button type="button" className="admin-btn-primary" onClick={openCreate}>
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">add</span> New property
        </button>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {loading ? (
        <p style={{ padding: 20, color: "var(--color-gray)" }}>Loadingâ€¦</p>
      ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">home_work</span>
          <h3>No properties yet</h3>
          <p>List properties for sale or rent.</p>
          <button type="button" className="admin-btn-primary" onClick={openCreate}>Add first property</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead><tr><th>Cover</th><th>Title</th><th>Location</th><th>Price</th><th>Beds</th><th>Status</th><th></th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.map((p) => (
                <tr key={p.id}>
                  <td>{p.images?.[0] ? (
                    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                    <img src={p.images[0]} alt="" style={{ width: 48, height: 48, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6, border: "1px solid var(--color-gray-border)" }} />
                  ) : (
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined" style={{ fontSize: 20, color: "var(--color-gray-mid)" }}>image</span>
                  )}</td>
                  <td><strong>{p.title}</strong></td>
                  <td>{p.location}</td>
                  <td>{formatPrice(p.price, p.currency)}</td>
                  <td>{p.bedrooms}/{p.bathrooms}</td>
                  <td><span className={`status-badge ${p.published ? "status-published" : "status-draft"}`}>{p.published ? "Published" : "Draft"}</span></td>
                  <td>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                      <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost admin-btn-small" onClick={() => openEdit(p)}>Edit</button>
                      <button type="button" className="admin-btn-danger admin-btn-small" onClick={() => del(p)}>Delete</button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {open && (
        <>
          <div className="admin-drawer-backdrop" onClick={closeDrawer} />
          <aside className="admin-drawer">
            <div className="admin-drawer-head">
              <h2>{editing ? "Edit property" : "New property"}</h2>
              <button type="button" className="admin-drawer-close" onClick={closeDrawer}><span className="material-symbols-outlined">close</span></button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={save} style={{ display: "contents" }}>
              <div className="admin-drawer-body">
                <div className="admin-form-fields">
                  <div className="admin-field"><label>Title</label>
                    <input type="text" value={draft.title} onChange={(e) => set("title", e.target.value)} required />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field"><label>Description</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.description} onChange={(e) => set("description", e.target.value)} rows={4} required />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Price</label>
                      <input type="number" value={draft.price} onChange={(e) => set("price", e.target.value)} required min={0} step="0.01" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Currency</label>
                      <select value={draft.currency} onChange={(e) => set("currency", e.target.value)}>
                        <option value="GHS">GHS</option><option value="USD">USD</option><option value="EUR">EUR</option><option value="GBP">GBP</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Status</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.status} onChange={(e) => set("status", e.target.value)} required placeholder="For Sale / For Rent" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Type</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.type} onChange={(e) => set("type", e.target.value)} required placeholder="Apartment / House" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Location</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.location} onChange={(e) => set("location", e.target.value)} required />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Style (optional)</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.style} onChange={(e) => set("style", e.target.value)} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Rooms</label>
                      <input type="number" value={draft.rooms} onChange={(e) => set("rooms", Number(e.target.value))} min={0} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Bedrooms</label>
                      <input type="number" value={draft.bedrooms} onChange={(e) => set("bedrooms", Number(e.target.value))} min={0} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Bathrooms</label>
                      <input type="number" value={draft.bathrooms} onChange={(e) => set("bathrooms", Number(e.target.value))} min={0} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field"><label>Parking</label>
                      <input type="number" value={draft.parking} onChange={(e) => set("parking", Number(e.target.value))} min={0} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field"><label>Area (sqft)</label>
                    <input type="number" value={draft.area} onChange={(e) => set("area", Number(e.target.value))} min={0} />
                  </div>
                  <ImagesField values={draft.images} onChange={(v) => set("images", v)} />
                  <div className="admin-field"><label>Amenities (one per line)</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.amenitiesText} onChange={(e) => set("amenitiesText", e.target.value)} rows={3} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field"><label>Options (one per line)</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.optionsText} onChange={(e) => set("optionsText", e.target.value)} rows={3} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field admin-field-inline">
                    <label><input type="checkbox" checked={draft.published} onChange={(e) => set("published", e.target.checked)} /> Published</label>
                  </div>
                  {saveError && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{saveError}</div>}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="admin-drawer-foot">
                <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost" onClick={closeDrawer} disabled={saving}>Cancel</button>
                <button type="submit" className="admin-btn-primary" disabled={saving}>
                  {saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : editing ? "Save changes" : "Create"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </aside>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import "@/styles/admin.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

type News = {
  id: string; slug: string; title: string; excerpt: string; content: string;
  coverImage: string; category: string; published: boolean; publishedAt: string | null;
};

type Draft = {
  title: string; slug: string; excerpt: string; content: string;
  coverImage: string; category: string; published: boolean; publishedAt: string;
};

const emptyDraft: Draft = {
  title: "", slug: "", excerpt: "", content: "",
  coverImage: "", category: "", published: false, publishedAt: "",
};

function slugify(s: string) {
  return s.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");
}

async function apiCall(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown) {
  const res = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, {
    method, credentials: "include",
    headers: body ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : undefined,
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });
  let json: any = null;
  try { json = await res.json(); } catch {}
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json?.message ?? `Failed (${res.status})`);
  return json;
}

async function uploadImage(file: File, folder: string): Promise<string> {
  const signRes = await fetch(`${API}/api/media/sign`, {
    method: "POST", credentials: "include",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ folder }),
  });
  if (!signRes.ok) {
    const e = await signRes.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Sign failed (${signRes.status}): ${e.slice(0, 150)}`);
  }
  const sig: any = await signRes.json();
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append("file", file);
  form.append("api_key", sig.apiKey);
  form.append("timestamp", String(sig.timestamp));
  form.append("signature", sig.signature);
  form.append("folder", sig.folder);
  const up = await fetch(
    `https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${sig.cloudName}/image/upload`,
    { method: "POST", body: form },
  );
  if (!up.ok) {
    const t = await up.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Upload failed (${up.status}): ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  return (await up.json()).secure_url;
}

function ImageField({
  label, value, onChange, required,
}: {
  label: string; value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void; required?: boolean;
}) {
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const onFile = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) { setError("Please choose an image"); return; }
    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError("Max 8MB"); return; }
    setError(null); setUploading(true);
    try { onChange(await uploadImage(file, "ted-nomex/news")); }
    catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Upload failed"); }
    finally { setUploading(false); if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = ""; }
  };

  return (
    <div className="admin-field admin-field-full">
      <label>{label}{required && <span style={{ color: "#b91c1c" }}> *</span>}</label>
      {value ? (
        <div className="admin-upload-preview">
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img src={value} alt="Preview" />
          <div className="admin-upload-actions">
            <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost admin-btn-small" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} disabled={uploading}>
              Replace
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="admin-btn-danger admin-btn-small" onClick={() => onChange("")} disabled={uploading}>
              Remove
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <button type="button" className="admin-upload-drop" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} disabled={uploading}>
          {uploading
            ? (<><span className="material-symbols-outlined">progress_activity</span> Uploadingâ€¦</>)
            : (<><span className="material-symbols-outlined">upload</span> Choose image from your machine</>)}
        </button>
      )}
      <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept="image/*" style={{ display: "none" }} onChange={onFile} />
      <p className="admin-field-hint">JPG, PNG or WebP, up to 8MB.</p>
      {error && <p className="admin-field-error">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AdminNewsPage() {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<News[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<News | null>(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Draft>(emptyDraft);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = async () => {
    setLoading(true); setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/cms/news/admin/all?published=all`, { credentials: "include" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed (${res.status})`);
      setRows(await res.json());
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load"); }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  };

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  const openCreate = () => { setEditing(null); setDraft(emptyDraft); setSaveError(null); setOpen(true); };
  const openEdit = (row: News) => {
    setEditing(row);
    setDraft({
      title: row.title, slug: row.slug, excerpt: row.excerpt, content: row.content,
      coverImage: row.coverImage, category: row.category, published: row.published,
      publishedAt: row.publishedAt ? new Date(row.publishedAt).toISOString().slice(0, 16) : "",
    });
    setSaveError(null); setOpen(true);
  };
  const closeDrawer = () => { if (saving) return; setOpen(false); setEditing(null); };
  const set = <K extends keyof Draft>(k: K, v: Draft[K]) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, [k]: v }));

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setSaving(true); setSaveError(null);
    try {
      const payload = {
        title: draft.title, slug: draft.slug || slugify(draft.title),
        excerpt: draft.excerpt, content: draft.content,
        coverImage: draft.coverImage, category: draft.category,
        published: draft.published, publishedAt: draft.publishedAt || null,
      };
      if (editing) await apiCall("PATCH", `/api/cms/news/${editing.id}`, payload);
      else await apiCall("POST", "/api/cms/news", payload);
      setOpen(false); setEditing(null); await load();
    } catch (err) { setSaveError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save"); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  const del = async (row: News) => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete "${row.title}"?`)) return;
    try { await apiCall("DELETE", `/api/cms/news/${row.id}`); await load(); }
    catch (err) { alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to delete"); }
  };

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div><h1>News</h1><p>{rows.length} total</p></div>
        <button type="button" className="admin-btn-primary" onClick={openCreate}>
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">add</span> New post
        </button>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {loading ? (
        <p style={{ padding: 20, color: "var(--color-gray)" }}>Loadingâ€¦</p>
      ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">newspaper</span>
          <h3>No news posts yet</h3>
          <p>Create your first article.</p>
          <button type="button" className="admin-btn-primary" onClick={openCreate}>Create first post</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead><tr><th>Cover</th><th>Title</th><th>Category</th><th>Status</th><th></th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.map((r) => (
                <tr key={r.id}>
                  <td>{r.coverImage ? (
                    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                    <img src={r.coverImage} alt="" style={{ width: 48, height: 48, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6, border: "1px solid var(--color-gray-border)" }} />
                  ) : (
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined" style={{ fontSize: 20, color: "var(--color-gray-mid)" }}>image</span>
                  )}</td>
                  <td><strong>{r.title}</strong></td>
                  <td>{r.category}</td>
                  <td><span className={`status-badge ${r.published ? "status-published" : "status-draft"}`}>{r.published ? "Published" : "Draft"}</span></td>
                  <td>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                      <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost admin-btn-small" onClick={() => openEdit(r)}>Edit</button>
                      <button type="button" className="admin-btn-danger admin-btn-small" onClick={() => del(r)}>Delete</button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {open && (
        <>
          <div className="admin-drawer-backdrop" onClick={closeDrawer} />
          <aside className="admin-drawer">
            <div className="admin-drawer-head">
              <h2>{editing ? "Edit news post" : "New news post"}</h2>
              <button type="button" className="admin-drawer-close" onClick={closeDrawer}><span className="material-symbols-outlined">close</span></button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={save} style={{ display: "contents" }}>
              <div className="admin-drawer-body">
                <div className="admin-form-fields">
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Title</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.title}
                        onChange={(e) => {
                          set("title", e.target.value);
                          if (!editing && !draft.slug) set("slug", slugify(e.target.value));
                        }}
                        required minLength={3} maxLength={200} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Slug</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.slug} onChange={(e) => set("slug", e.target.value)} required pattern="[a-z0-9-]+" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Category</label>
                    <input type="text" value={draft.category} onChange={(e) => set("category", e.target.value)} required />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Excerpt</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.excerpt} onChange={(e) => set("excerpt", e.target.value)} rows={2} required minLength={10} maxLength={500} />
                  </div>
                  <ImageField label="Cover image" value={draft.coverImage} onChange={(v) => set("coverImage", v)} required />
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Content</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.content} onChange={(e) => set("content", e.target.value)} rows={12} required minLength={20} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field admin-field-inline">
                    <label><input type="checkbox" checked={draft.published} onChange={(e) => set("published", e.target.checked)} /> Published</label>
                  </div>
                  {draft.published && (
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Published at</label>
                      <input type="datetime-local" value={draft.publishedAt} onChange={(e) => set("publishedAt", e.target.value)} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {saveError && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{saveError}</div>}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="admin-drawer-foot">
                <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost" onClick={closeDrawer} disabled={saving}>Cancel</button>
                <button type="submit" className="admin-btn-primary" disabled={saving}>
                  {saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : editing ? "Save changes" : "Create"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </aside>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { getAdminOrders } from "@/lib/admin";
import { StatusBadge, orderVariant } from "@/components/admin/StatusBadge";
import "@/styles/admin.css";

export const metadata = { title: "Admin — Orders" };

function formatMoney(amount: string, currency: string) {
  const n = Number(amount);
  const symbol = currency === "GHS" ? "GH₵" : currency;
  return `${symbol} ${n.toLocaleString()}`;
}

function formatDate(iso: string) {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}

export default async function AdminOrdersPage() {
  let orders: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getAdminOrders>> = [];
  let error: string | null = null;
  try {
    orders = await getAdminOrders();
  } catch (err) {
    error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not load orders";
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div>
          <h1>Orders</h1>
          <p>{orders.length} total</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {orders.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">shopping_bag</span>
          <h3>No orders yet</h3>
          <p>Customer orders will appear here once someone checks out.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Order</th>
                <th>Customer</th>
                <th>Items</th>
                <th>Total</th>
                <th>Status</th>
                <th>Date</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {orders.map((o) => (
                <tr key={o.id}>
                  <td>
                    <code>{o.id.slice(0, 8)}</code>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="cell-stack">
                      <strong>{o.user?.name ?? o.email}</strong>
                      <span>{o.email}</span>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td>{o.items.length}</td>
                  <td>{formatMoney(o.total, o.currency)}</td>
                  <td>
                    <StatusBadge variant={orderVariant(o.status)} />
                  </td>
                  <td>{formatDate(o.createdAt)}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import {
  getAdminNews,
  getAdminProperties,
  getAdminProducts,
  getAdminServices,
  getAdminQuotes,
  getAdminOrders,
  getAdminUsers,
} from "@/lib/admin";
import "@/styles/admin.css";

export const metadata = { title: "Admin â€” Overview" };

async function safe<T>(fn: () => Promise<T[]>): Promise<T[]> {
  try { return await fn(); } catch { return []; }
}

export default async function AdminOverviewPage() {
  const [news, properties, products, services, quotes, orders, users] =
    await Promise.all([
      safe(getAdminNews),
      safe(getAdminProperties),
      safe(getAdminProducts),
      safe(getAdminServices),
      safe(getAdminQuotes),
      safe(getAdminOrders),
      safe(getAdminUsers),
    ]);

  const stats = [
    { label: "News", href: "/admin/news", value: news.length, icon: "newspaper" },
    { label: "Properties", href: "/admin/estate", value: properties.length, icon: "home_work" },
    { label: "Products", href: "/admin/products", value: products.length, icon: "inventory_2" },
    { label: "Services", href: "/admin/services", value: services.length, icon: "handyman" },
    { label: "Quotes", href: "/admin/quotes", value: quotes.length, icon: "request_quote" },
    { label: "Orders", href: "/admin/orders", value: orders.length, icon: "shopping_bag" },
    { label: "Users", href: "/admin/users", value: users.length, icon: "group" },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="admin-overview">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div>
          <h1>Overview</h1>
          <p>Content, orders and users across the site.</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="admin-grid">
        {stats.map((s) => (
          <a key={s.label} href={s.href} className="admin-stat">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">{s.icon}</span>
            <p className="label">{s.label}</p>
            <p className="value">{s.value}</p>
          </a>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import "@/styles/admin.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

type Product = {
  id: string; slug: string; name: string; description: string;
  price: string; currency: string; stock: number; images: string[];
  category: string | null; published: boolean;
};

type Draft = {
  name: string; slug: string; description: string; price: string;
  currency: string; stock: number; images: string[]; category: string; published: boolean;
};

const emptyDraft: Draft = {
  name: "", slug: "", description: "", price: "0",
  currency: "GHS", stock: 0, images: [], category: "", published: true,
};

function slugify(s: string) {
  return s.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");
}

async function apiCall(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown) {
  const res = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, {
    method, credentials: "include",
    headers: body ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : undefined,
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });
  let json: any = null;
  try { json = await res.json(); } catch {}
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json?.message ?? `Failed (${res.status})`);
  return json;
}

async function uploadImage(file: File, folder: string): Promise<string> {
  const signRes = await fetch(`${API}/api/media/sign`, {
    method: "POST", credentials: "include",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ folder }),
  });
  if (!signRes.ok) {
    const e = await signRes.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Sign failed (${signRes.status}): ${e.slice(0, 150)}`);
  }
  const sig: any = await signRes.json();
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append("file", file);
  form.append("api_key", sig.apiKey);
  form.append("timestamp", String(sig.timestamp));
  form.append("signature", sig.signature);
  form.append("folder", sig.folder);
  const up = await fetch(`https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${sig.cloudName}/image/upload`, { method: "POST", body: form });
  if (!up.ok) {
    const t = await up.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Upload failed (${up.status}): ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  return (await up.json()).secure_url;
}

function ImagesField({ values, onChange }: { values: string[]; onChange: (v: string[]) => void }) {
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const onFiles = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const files = Array.from(e.target.files ?? []);
    if (files.length === 0) return;
    setError(null); setUploading(true);
    const next = [...values];
    try {
      for (const file of files) {
        if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) continue;
        if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) continue;
        next.push(await uploadImage(file, "ted-nomex/products"));
      }
      onChange(next);
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Upload failed"); }
    finally { setUploading(false); if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = ""; }
  };

  return (
    <div className="admin-field admin-field-full">
      <label>Images</label>
      <div className="admin-upload-grid">
        {values.map((url, i) => (
          <div key={`${url}-${i}`} className="admin-upload-thumb">
            {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
            <img src={url} alt={`Image ${i + 1}`} />
            <button type="button" className="admin-upload-thumb-remove" onClick={() => onChange(values.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i))} aria-label="Remove">
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">close</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
        <button type="button" className="admin-upload-add" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} disabled={uploading}>
          {uploading ? (<span className="material-symbols-outlined">progress_activity</span>) : (<><span className="material-symbols-outlined">add_photo_alternate</span>Add</>)}
        </button>
      </div>
      <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept="image/*" multiple style={{ display: "none" }} onChange={onFiles} />
      <p className="admin-field-hint">JPG, PNG or WebP, up to 8MB each.</p>
      {error && <p className="admin-field-error">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AdminProductsPage() {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Product | null>(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Draft>(emptyDraft);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = async () => {
    setLoading(true); setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/shop/products/admin/all`, { credentials: "include" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed (${res.status})`);
      setRows(await res.json());
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load"); }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  };

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  const openCreate = () => { setEditing(null); setDraft(emptyDraft); setSaveError(null); setOpen(true); };
  const openEdit = (row: Product) => {
    setEditing(row);
    setDraft({
      name: row.name, slug: row.slug, description: row.description,
      price: String(row.price), currency: row.currency, stock: row.stock,
      images: row.images ?? [], category: row.category ?? "", published: row.published,
    });
    setSaveError(null); setOpen(true);
  };
  const closeDrawer = () => { if (saving) return; setOpen(false); setEditing(null); };
  const set = <K extends keyof Draft>(k: K, v: Draft[K]) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, [k]: v }));

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setSaving(true); setSaveError(null);
    try {
      const payload = {
        name: draft.name, slug: draft.slug || slugify(draft.name),
        description: draft.description,
        price: Number(draft.price), currency: draft.currency,
        stock: Number(draft.stock), images: draft.images,
        category: draft.category || null, published: draft.published,
      };
      if (editing) await apiCall("PATCH", `/api/shop/products/${editing.id}`, payload);
      else await apiCall("POST", "/api/shop/products", payload);
      setOpen(false); setEditing(null); await load();
    } catch (err) { setSaveError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save"); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  const del = async (row: Product) => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete "${row.name}"?`)) return;
    try { await apiCall("DELETE", `/api/shop/products/${row.id}`); await load(); }
    catch (err) { alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to delete"); }
  };

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div><h1>Products</h1><p>{rows.length} total</p></div>
        <button type="button" className="admin-btn-primary" onClick={openCreate}>
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">add</span> New product
        </button>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {loading ? (
        <p style={{ padding: 20, color: "var(--color-gray)" }}>Loadingâ€¦</p>
      ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">inventory_2</span>
          <h3>No products yet</h3>
          <p>Add shop products and track stock.</p>
          <button type="button" className="admin-btn-primary" onClick={openCreate}>Add first product</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead><tr><th>Cover</th><th>Name</th><th>Category</th><th>Price</th><th>Stock</th><th>Status</th><th></th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.map((p) => (
                <tr key={p.id}>
                  <td>{p.images?.[0] ? (
                    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                    <img src={p.images[0]} alt="" style={{ width: 48, height: 48, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6, border: "1px solid var(--color-gray-border)" }} />
                  ) : (
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined" style={{ fontSize: 20, color: "var(--color-gray-mid)" }}>image</span>
                  )}</td>
                  <td><strong>{p.name}</strong></td>
                  <td>{p.category ?? "â€”"}</td>
                  <td>{p.currency === "GHS" ? "GHâ‚µ" : p.currency} {Number(p.price).toLocaleString()}</td>
                  <td>{p.stock}</td>
                  <td><span className={`status-badge ${p.published ? "status-published" : "status-draft"}`}>{p.published ? "Published" : "Draft"}</span></td>
                  <td>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                      <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost admin-btn-small" onClick={() => openEdit(p)}>Edit</button>
                      <button type="button" className="admin-btn-danger admin-btn-small" onClick={() => del(p)}>Delete</button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {open && (
        <>
          <div className="admin-drawer-backdrop" onClick={closeDrawer} />
          <aside className="admin-drawer">
            <div className="admin-drawer-head">
              <h2>{editing ? "Edit product" : "New product"}</h2>
              <button type="button" className="admin-drawer-close" onClick={closeDrawer}><span className="material-symbols-outlined">close</span></button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={save} style={{ display: "contents" }}>
              <div className="admin-drawer-body">
                <div className="admin-form-fields">
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Name</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.name}
                        onChange={(e) => {
                          set("name", e.target.value);
                          if (!editing && !draft.slug) set("slug", slugify(e.target.value));
                        }}
                        required minLength={3} maxLength={200} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Slug</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.slug} onChange={(e) => set("slug", e.target.value)} required pattern="[a-z0-9-]+" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Description</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.description} onChange={(e) => set("description", e.target.value)} rows={4} required />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Price</label>
                      <input type="number" value={draft.price} onChange={(e) => set("price", e.target.value)} required min={0} step="0.01" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Currency</label>
                      <select value={draft.currency} onChange={(e) => set("currency", e.target.value)}>
                        <option value="GHS">GHS</option><option value="USD">USD</option><option value="EUR">EUR</option><option value="GBP">GBP</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Stock</label>
                      <input type="number" value={draft.stock} onChange={(e) => set("stock", Number(e.target.value))} min={0} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Category (optional)</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.category} onChange={(e) => set("category", e.target.value)} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <ImagesField values={draft.images} onChange={(v) => set("images", v)} />
                  <div className="admin-field admin-field-inline">
                    <label><input type="checkbox" checked={draft.published} onChange={(e) => set("published", e.target.checked)} /> Published</label>
                  </div>
                  {saveError && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{saveError}</div>}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="admin-drawer-foot">
                <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost" onClick={closeDrawer} disabled={saving}>Cancel</button>
                <button type="submit" className="admin-btn-primary" disabled={saving}>
                  {saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : editing ? "Save changes" : "Create"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </aside>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { getAdminQuotes } from "@/lib/admin";
import { StatusBadge, quoteVariant } from "@/components/admin/StatusBadge";
import "@/styles/admin.css";

export const metadata = { title: "Admin — Quotes" };

export default async function AdminQuotesPage() {
  let quotes: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getAdminQuotes>> = [];
  let error: string | null = null;
  try {
    quotes = await getAdminQuotes();
  } catch (err) {
    error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not load quotes";
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div>
          <h1>Quotes</h1>
          <p>{quotes.length} total</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {quotes.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">request_quote</span>
          <h3>No quotes yet</h3>
          <p>Quote calculator submissions will show up here.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Name</th>
                <th>Email</th>
                <th>Estimate</th>
                <th>Status</th>
                <th>Date</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {quotes.map((q) => (
                <tr key={q.id}>
                  <td>
                    <strong>{q.name}</strong>
                  </td>
                  <td>{q.email}</td>
                  <td>
                    {q.currency === "GHS" ? "GH₵" : q.currency}{" "}
                    {Number(q.estimate).toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <StatusBadge variant={quoteVariant(q.status)} />
                  </td>
                  <td>{new Date(q.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("en-GB")}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import "@/styles/admin.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

type Service = {
  id: string; slug: string; title: string; excerpt: string; content: string;
  coverImage: string; features: string[]; tags: string[]; bullets: string[];
  order: number; published: boolean;
};

type Draft = {
  title: string; slug: string; excerpt: string; content: string;
  coverImage: string; featuresText: string; tagsText: string; bulletsText: string;
  order: number; published: boolean;
};

const emptyDraft: Draft = {
  title: "", slug: "", excerpt: "", content: "", coverImage: "",
  featuresText: "", tagsText: "", bulletsText: "", order: 0, published: true,
};

function slugify(s: string) {
  return s.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");
}
function splitLines(s: string) {
  return s.split(/\r?\n|,/).map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

async function apiCall(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown) {
  const res = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, {
    method, credentials: "include",
    headers: body ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : undefined,
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });
  let json: any = null;
  try { json = await res.json(); } catch {}
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json?.message ?? `Failed (${res.status})`);
  return json;
}

async function uploadImage(file: File, folder: string): Promise<string> {
  const signRes = await fetch(`${API}/api/media/sign`, {
    method: "POST", credentials: "include",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ folder }),
  });
  if (!signRes.ok) {
    const e = await signRes.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Sign failed (${signRes.status}): ${e.slice(0, 150)}`);
  }
  const sig: any = await signRes.json();
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append("file", file);
  form.append("api_key", sig.apiKey);
  form.append("timestamp", String(sig.timestamp));
  form.append("signature", sig.signature);
  form.append("folder", sig.folder);
  const up = await fetch(`https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${sig.cloudName}/image/upload`, { method: "POST", body: form });
  if (!up.ok) {
    const t = await up.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Upload failed (${up.status}): ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  return (await up.json()).secure_url;
}

function ImageField({ value, onChange }: { value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void }) {
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const onFile = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) { setError("Please choose an image"); return; }
    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError("Max 8MB"); return; }
    setError(null); setUploading(true);
    try { onChange(await uploadImage(file, "ted-nomex/services")); }
    catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Upload failed"); }
    finally { setUploading(false); if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = ""; }
  };

  return (
    <div className="admin-field admin-field-full">
      <label>Cover image <span style={{ color: "#b91c1c" }}>*</span></label>
      {value ? (
        <div className="admin-upload-preview">
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img src={value} alt="Preview" />
          <div className="admin-upload-actions">
            <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost admin-btn-small" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} disabled={uploading}>Replace</button>
            <button type="button" className="admin-btn-danger admin-btn-small" onClick={() => onChange("")} disabled={uploading}>Remove</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <button type="button" className="admin-upload-drop" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} disabled={uploading}>
          {uploading
            ? (<><span className="material-symbols-outlined">progress_activity</span> Uploadingâ€¦</>)
            : (<><span className="material-symbols-outlined">upload</span> Choose image from your machine</>)}
        </button>
      )}
      <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept="image/*" style={{ display: "none" }} onChange={onFile} />
      <p className="admin-field-hint">JPG, PNG or WebP, up to 8MB.</p>
      {error && <p className="admin-field-error">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AdminServicesPage() {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Service[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Service | null>(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Draft>(emptyDraft);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = async () => {
    setLoading(true); setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/cms/services/admin/all`, { credentials: "include" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed (${res.status})`);
      setRows(await res.json());
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load"); }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  };

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  const openCreate = () => { setEditing(null); setDraft(emptyDraft); setSaveError(null); setOpen(true); };
  const openEdit = (row: Service) => {
    setEditing(row);
    setDraft({
      title: row.title, slug: row.slug, excerpt: row.excerpt, content: row.content,
      coverImage: row.coverImage,
      featuresText: (row.features ?? []).join("\n"),
      tagsText: (row.tags ?? []).join("\n"),
      bulletsText: (row.bullets ?? []).join("\n"),
      order: row.order, published: row.published,
    });
    setSaveError(null); setOpen(true);
  };
  const closeDrawer = () => { if (saving) return; setOpen(false); setEditing(null); };
  const set = <K extends keyof Draft>(k: K, v: Draft[K]) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, [k]: v }));

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setSaving(true); setSaveError(null);
    try {
      const payload = {
        title: draft.title, slug: draft.slug || slugify(draft.title),
        excerpt: draft.excerpt, content: draft.content,
        coverImage: draft.coverImage,
        features: splitLines(draft.featuresText),
        tags: splitLines(draft.tagsText),
        bullets: splitLines(draft.bulletsText),
        order: Number(draft.order), published: draft.published,
      };
      if (editing) await apiCall("PATCH", `/api/cms/services/${editing.id}`, payload);
      else await apiCall("POST", "/api/cms/services", payload);
      setOpen(false); setEditing(null); await load();
    } catch (err) { setSaveError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save"); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  const del = async (row: Service) => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete "${row.title}"?`)) return;
    try { await apiCall("DELETE", `/api/cms/services/${row.id}`); await load(); }
    catch (err) { alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to delete"); }
  };

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div><h1>Services</h1><p>{rows.length} total</p></div>
        <button type="button" className="admin-btn-primary" onClick={openCreate}>
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">add</span> New service
        </button>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {loading ? (
        <p style={{ padding: 20, color: "var(--color-gray)" }}>Loadingâ€¦</p>
      ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">handyman</span>
          <h3>No services yet</h3>
          <p>Add the services you offer.</p>
          <button type="button" className="admin-btn-primary" onClick={openCreate}>Add first service</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead><tr><th>Cover</th><th>Title</th><th>Order</th><th>Status</th><th></th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.map((s) => (
                <tr key={s.id}>
                  <td>{s.coverImage ? (
                    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                    <img src={s.coverImage} alt="" style={{ width: 48, height: 48, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6, border: "1px solid var(--color-gray-border)" }} />
                  ) : (
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined" style={{ fontSize: 20, color: "var(--color-gray-mid)" }}>image</span>
                  )}</td>
                  <td><strong>{s.title}</strong></td>
                  <td>{s.order}</td>
                  <td><span className={`status-badge ${s.published ? "status-published" : "status-draft"}`}>{s.published ? "Published" : "Draft"}</span></td>
                  <td>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                      <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost admin-btn-small" onClick={() => openEdit(s)}>Edit</button>
                      <button type="button" className="admin-btn-danger admin-btn-small" onClick={() => del(s)}>Delete</button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {open && (
        <>
          <div className="admin-drawer-backdrop" onClick={closeDrawer} />
          <aside className="admin-drawer">
            <div className="admin-drawer-head">
              <h2>{editing ? "Edit service" : "New service"}</h2>
              <button type="button" className="admin-drawer-close" onClick={closeDrawer}><span className="material-symbols-outlined">close</span></button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={save} style={{ display: "contents" }}>
              <div className="admin-drawer-body">
                <div className="admin-form-fields">
                  <div className="admin-field-row">
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Title</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.title}
                        onChange={(e) => {
                          set("title", e.target.value);
                          if (!editing && !draft.slug) set("slug", slugify(e.target.value));
                        }}
                        required />
                    </div>
                    <div className="admin-field">
                      <label>Slug</label>
                      <input type="text" value={draft.slug} onChange={(e) => set("slug", e.target.value)} required pattern="[a-z0-9-]+" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Excerpt</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.excerpt} onChange={(e) => set("excerpt", e.target.value)} rows={2} required />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Content</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.content} onChange={(e) => set("content", e.target.value)} rows={8} required />
                  </div>
                  <ImageField value={draft.coverImage} onChange={(v) => set("coverImage", v)} />
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Features (one per line)</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.featuresText} onChange={(e) => set("featuresText", e.target.value)} rows={4} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Tags (one per line)</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.tagsText} onChange={(e) => set("tagsText", e.target.value)} rows={3} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Bullets (one per line)</label>
                    <textarea value={draft.bulletsText} onChange={(e) => set("bulletsText", e.target.value)} rows={4} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field">
                    <label>Order</label>
                    <input type="number" value={draft.order} onChange={(e) => set("order", Number(e.target.value))} min={0} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="admin-field admin-field-inline">
                    <label><input type="checkbox" checked={draft.published} onChange={(e) => set("published", e.target.checked)} /> Published</label>
                  </div>
                  {saveError && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{saveError}</div>}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="admin-drawer-foot">
                <button type="button" className="admin-btn-ghost" onClick={closeDrawer} disabled={saving}>Cancel</button>
                <button type="submit" className="admin-btn-primary" disabled={saving}>
                  {saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : editing ? "Save changes" : "Create"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </aside>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { getAdminUsers } from "@/lib/admin";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/admin/StatusBadge";
import "@/styles/admin.css";

export const metadata = { title: "Admin â€” Users" };

export default async function AdminUsersPage() {
  let users: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getAdminUsers>> = [];
  let error: string | null = null;
  try {
    users = await getAdminUsers();
  } catch (err) {
    error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not load users";
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div>
          <h1>Users</h1>
          <p>{users.length} registered</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {users.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">group</span>
          <h3>No users yet</h3>
          <p>Registered accounts will appear here.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Avatar</th>
                <th>Name</th>
                <th>Email</th>
                <th>Role</th>
                <th>Verified</th>
                <th>Joined</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {users.map((u) => {
                const initial = (u.name ?? u.email ?? "U")
                  .trim()
                  .charAt(0)
                  .toUpperCase();
                return (
                  <tr key={u.id}>
                    <td>
                      <div className="user-avatar">
                        {u.image ? (
                          // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                          <img src={u.image} alt={u.name ?? u.email} />
                        ) : (
                          <span>{initial}</span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                      <strong>{u.name ?? "â€”"}</strong>
                    </td>
                    <td>{u.email}</td>
                    <td>
                      <span className={`role-pill role-${u.role.toLowerCase()}`}>
                        {u.role}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                      <StatusBadge
                        variant={u.emailVerified ? "published" : "draft"}
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td>{new Date(u.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("en-GB")}</td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { getSession } from "@/modules/auth/session";
import { AdminShell } from "@/components/admin/Shell";
export const instant = false;
export default async function AdminLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const user = await getSession();
  if (!user) redirect("/login");
  if (user.role !== "ADMIN") redirect("/dashboard");

  return <AdminShell>{children}</AdminShell>;
}

FILE: app\(auth)\forgot-password\page.tsx
════════════════════════════════════════════
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { Suspense, useState } from "react";
import { requestPasswordReset, resetPassword } from "@/modules/auth/session";
import { PasswordInput } from "@/components/auth/PasswordInput";

// â”€â”€ Step 1: request email â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function RequestForm() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);
  const [devToken, setDevToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const onSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await requestPasswordReset(email);
      setSent(true);
      if (result.devToken) setDevToken(result.devToken);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not send reset link");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="auth-form">
      <h1>Forgot password</h1>
      <p className="muted">
        Enter your email and we&apos;ll send you a reset link.
      </p>

      {!sent && (
        <>
          <label htmlFor="email">Email</label>
          <input
            id="email"
            type="email"
            value={email}
            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
            required
            autoComplete="email"
            disabled={loading}
          />
          {error && <p className="form-error">{error}</p>}
          <button type="submit" className="btn-primary" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Sending\u2026" : "Send reset link"}
          </button>
        </>
      )}

      {sent && (
        <>
          <p className="form-success">
            If an account exists for <strong>{email}</strong>, a reset link will
            arrive shortly.
          </p>

          {devToken && (
            <div className="form-hint">
              <strong>Dev mode:</strong> Resend isn&apos;t delivering emails yet.
              Click to test the reset form now:
              <br />
              <Link
                href={`/forgot-password?token=${encodeURIComponent(devToken)}`}
                style={{
                  display: "inline-block",
                  marginTop: 8,
                  color: "var(--color-primary)",
                  fontWeight: 700,
                }}
              >
                â†’ Continue to password reset
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      <p className="form-footer">
        <Link href="/login">Back to sign in</Link>
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}

// â”€â”€ Step 2: enter new password (same page, ?token= in URL) â”€
function ResetForm({ token }: { token: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  const onSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (password.length < 8) {
      setError("Password must be at least 8 characters");
      return;
    }
    if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(password)) {
      setError("Password must contain a letter");
      return;
    }
    if (!/\d/.test(password)) {
      setError("Password must contain a number");
      return;
    }
    if (password !== confirm) {
      setError("Passwords do not match");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await resetPassword(token, password);
      setDone(true);
      setTimeout(() => router.push("/login"), 2200);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not reset password");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (done) {
    return (
      <div className="auth-form">
        <h1>Password updated</h1>
        <p className="form-success">
          Your password has been changed. Redirecting to sign inâ€¦
        </p>
        <p className="form-footer">
          <Link href="/login">Sign in now</Link>
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="auth-form">
      <h1>Choose a new password</h1>
      <p className="muted">
        Your reset link is valid. Enter a strong new password below.
      </p>

      <label htmlFor="password">New password</label>
      <PasswordInput
        id="password"
        value={password}
        onChange={setPassword}
        autoComplete="new-password"
        required
        minLength={8}
        disabled={loading}
      />
      <p className="form-hint-inline">
        At least 8 characters, including a letter and a number.
      </p>

      <label htmlFor="confirm">Confirm password</label>
      <PasswordInput
        id="confirm"
        value={confirm}
        onChange={setConfirm}
        autoComplete="new-password"
        required
        minLength={8}
        disabled={loading}
      />

      {error && <p className="form-error">{error}</p>}

      <button type="submit" className="btn-primary" disabled={loading}>
        {loading ? "Resetting\u2026" : "Reset password"}
      </button>

      <p className="form-footer">
        <Link href="/login">Back to sign in</Link>
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}

// â”€â”€ Router: choose step based on ?token= â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function ForgotPasswordRouter() {
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const token = params.get("token");
  if (token) return <ResetForm token={token} />;
  return <RequestForm />;
}

export default function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="auth-form">
          <p className="muted">Loadingâ€¦</p>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <ForgotPasswordRouter />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const onSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const user = await login(email, password);
      router.push(user.role === "ADMIN" ? "/admin" : "/dashboard");
      router.refresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Login failed");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="auth-form">
      <h1>Welcome back</h1>
      <p className="muted">Sign in to your Ted-Nomex account.</p>

      <label htmlFor="email">Email</label>
      <input
        id="email"
        type="email"
        autoComplete="email"
        value={email}
        onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
        required
      />

      <label htmlFor="password">Password</label>
      <PasswordInput
        id="password"
        value={password}
        onChange={setPassword}
        autoComplete="current-password"
        required
      />

      {error && (
        <p className="form-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      <button type="submit" className="btn-primary" disabled={loading}>
        {loading ? "Signing inâ€¦" : "Sign in"}
      </button>

      <div className="auth-divider">
        <span>or</span>
      </div>

      <a href={getGoogleAuthUrl()} className="auth-google">
        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">g_translate</span>
        Continue with Google
      </a>

      <p className="form-footer">
        <Link href="/forgot-password">Forgot password?</Link>
      </p>
      <p className="form-footer">
        Don&apos;t have an account? <Link href="/register">Create one</Link>
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import { register, getGoogleAuthUrl } from "@/modules/auth/session";
import { PasswordInput } from "@/components/auth/PasswordInput";

function validatePassword(pw: string): string | null {
  if (pw.length < 8) return "Password must be at least 8 characters";
  if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(pw)) return "Password must contain a letter";
  if (!/\d/.test(pw)) return "Password must contain a number";
  return null;
}

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const onSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    const pwErr = validatePassword(password);
    if (pwErr) {
      setError(pwErr);
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await register(name, email, password);
      router.push("/dashboard");
      router.refresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Registration failed");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="auth-form">
      <h1>Create account</h1>
      <p className="muted">
        Join Ted-Nomex to manage orders, quotes and projects.
      </p>

      <label htmlFor="name">Full name</label>
      <input
        id="name"
        autoComplete="name"
        value={name}
        onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
        required
        minLength={2}
        maxLength={80}
      />

      <label htmlFor="email">Email</label>
      <input
        id="email"
        type="email"
        autoComplete="email"
        value={email}
        onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
        required
      />

      <label htmlFor="password">Password</label>
      <PasswordInput
        id="password"
        value={password}
        onChange={setPassword}
        autoComplete="new-password"
        required
        minLength={8}
      />
      <p className="form-hint-inline">
        At least 8 characters, must include a letter and a number.
      </p>

      {error && (
        <p className="form-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      <button type="submit" className="btn-primary" disabled={loading}>
        {loading ? "Creating accountâ€¦" : "Create account"}
      </button>

      <div className="auth-divider">
        <span>or</span>
      </div>

      <a href={getGoogleAuthUrl()} className="auth-google">
        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">g_translate</span>
        Continue with Google
      </a>

      <p className="form-footer">
        Already registered? <Link href="/login">Sign in</Link>
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}
