import Link from "next/link";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/admin/StatusBadge";
import "@/styles/dashboard.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

async function fetchWithAuth<T>(path: string): Promise<T> {
  const { cookies } = await import("next/headers");
  const store = await cookies();
  const cookieHeader = store.getAll().map((c) => `${c.name}=${c.value}`).join("; ");
  const res = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, {
    headers: cookieHeader ? { cookie: cookieHeader } : undefined,
    cache: "no-store",
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed (${res.status})`);
  return res.json();
}

export default async function DashboardEnquiriesPage() {
  let enquiries: any[] = [];
  let error: string | null = null;
  try {
    enquiries = await fetchWithAuth<any[]>("/api/enquiries/mine");
  } catch (err) {
    error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not load enquiries";
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div>
          <h1>My Enquiries</h1>
          <p>{enquiries.length} total</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {enquiries.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">mail</span>
          <h3>No enquiries yet</h3>
          <p>Your messages to the Ted-Nomex team will appear here.</p>
          <Link href="/contact" className="admin-btn-primary">
            Contact us
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Subject</th>
                <th>Message</th>
                <th>Status</th>
                <th>Date</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {enquiries.map((e) => (
                <tr key={e.id}>
                  <td>
                    <strong>{e.subject ?? "(no subject)"}</strong>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div
                      style={{
                        maxWidth: 400,
                        overflow: "hidden",
                        textOverflow: "ellipsis",
                        whiteSpace: "nowrap",
                      }}
                    >
                      {e.message}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <StatusBadge variant={e.handled ? "handled" : "unhandled"} />
                  </td>
                  <td>{new Date(e.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("en-GB")}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { StatusBadge, orderVariant } from "@/components/admin/StatusBadge";
import "@/styles/dashboard.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

async function fetchWithAuth<T>(path: string): Promise<T> {
  const { cookies } = await import("next/headers");
  const store = await cookies();
  const cookieHeader = store.getAll().map((c) => `${c.name}=${c.value}`).join("; ");
  const res = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, {
    headers: cookieHeader ? { cookie: cookieHeader } : undefined,
    cache: "no-store",
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed (${res.status})`);
  return res.json();
}

function formatMoney(amount: string, currency: string) {
  const symbol = currency === "GHS" ? "GHâ‚µ" : currency;
  return `${symbol} ${Number(amount).toLocaleString()}`;
}
function formatDate(iso: string) {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}

export default async function DashboardOrdersPage() {
  let orders: any[] = [];
  let error: string | null = null;
  try {
    orders = await fetchWithAuth<any[]>("/api/orders/mine");
  } catch (err) {
    error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not load orders";
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div>
          <h1>My Orders</h1>
          <p>{orders.length} total</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {orders.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">shopping_bag</span>
          <h3>No orders yet</h3>
          <p>Your orders will appear here once you&apos;ve purchased from our shop.</p>
          <Link href="/product" className="admin-btn-primary">
            Browse the shop
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Order</th>
                <th>Items</th>
                <th>Total</th>
                <th>Status</th>
                <th>Payment</th>
                <th>Date</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {orders.map((o) => (
                <tr key={o.id}>
                  <td>
                    <code>{o.id.slice(0, 8)}</code>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="cell-stack">
                      {o.items.slice(0, 2).map((item: any) => (
                        <span key={item.id}>
                          {item.quantity}Ã— {item.title}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                      {o.items.length > 2 && <span>+{o.items.length - 2} more</span>}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td>{formatMoney(o.total, o.currency)}</td>
                  <td>
                    <StatusBadge variant={orderVariant(o.status)} />
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span
                      className={`status-badge ${
                        o.paymentStatus === "PAID"
                          ? "status-paid"
                          : o.paymentStatus === "FAILED"
                          ? "status-cancelled"
                          : "status-pending"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {o.paymentStatus}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td>{formatDate(o.createdAt)}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: app\(dashboard)\dashboard\page.tsx
════════════════════════════════════════════
import Link from "next/link";
import "@/styles/dashboard.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

async function fetchWithAuth<T>(path: string): Promise<T> {
  const { cookies } = await import("next/headers");
  const store = await cookies();
  const cookieHeader = store.getAll().map((c) => `${c.name}=${c.value}`).join("; ");
  const res = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, {
    headers: cookieHeader ? { cookie: cookieHeader } : undefined,
    cache: "no-store",
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed (${res.status})`);
  return res.json();
}

async function safe<T>(fn: () => Promise<T[]>): Promise<T[]> {
  try {
    const r = await fn();
    return Array.isArray(r) ? r : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export default async function DashboardOverviewPage() {
  const [orders, quotes, projects, enquiries] = await Promise.all([
    safe(() => fetchWithAuth<any[]>("/api/orders/mine")),
    safe(() => fetchWithAuth<any[]>("/api/quotes/mine")),
    safe(() => fetchWithAuth<any[]>("/api/projects/mine")),
    safe(() => fetchWithAuth<any[]>("/api/enquiries/mine")),
  ]);

  const openQuotes = quotes.filter((q) => q.status === "NEW" || q.status === "CONTACTED");
  const activeProjects = projects.filter(
    (p) => p.status === "IN_PROGRESS" || p.status === "PLANNING"
  );
  const openEnquiries = enquiries.filter((e) => !e.handled);

  const cards = [
    { title: "Total orders", value: orders.length, icon: "shopping_bag", href: "/dashboard/orders" },
    { title: "Open quotes", value: openQuotes.length, icon: "request_quote", href: "/dashboard/quotes", hint: `${quotes.length} total` },
    { title: "Active projects", value: activeProjects.length, icon: "apartment", href: "/dashboard/projects", hint: `${projects.length} total` },
    { title: "Enquiries", value: enquiries.length, icon: "mail", href: "/dashboard/enquiries", hint: `${openEnquiries.length} unanswered` },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="dash-overview">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div>
          <h1>Overview</h1>
          <p>Your orders, quotes, projects and messages in one place.</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="dash-grid">
        {cards.map((c) => (
          <Link key={c.title} href={c.href} className="stat-card">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">{c.icon}</span>
            <p className="stat-title">{c.title}</p>
            <p className="stat-value">{c.value}</p>
            {c.hint && <p className="stat-hint">{c.hint}</p>}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app\(dashboard)\dashboard\quotes\page.tsx
════════════════════════════════════════════
import Link from "next/link";
import { StatusBadge, quoteVariant } from "@/components/admin/StatusBadge";
import "@/styles/dashboard.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

async function fetchWithAuth<T>(path: string): Promise<T> {
  const { cookies } = await import("next/headers");
  const store = await cookies();
  const cookieHeader = store.getAll().map((c) => `${c.name}=${c.value}`).join("; ");
  const res = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, {
    headers: cookieHeader ? { cookie: cookieHeader } : undefined,
    cache: "no-store",
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed (${res.status})`);
  return res.json();
}

export default async function DashboardQuotesPage() {
  let quotes: any[] = [];
  let error: string | null = null;
  try {
    quotes = await fetchWithAuth<any[]>("/api/quotes/mine");
  } catch (err) {
    error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not load quotes";
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-page">
      <header className="admin-page-head">
        <div>
          <h1>My Quotes</h1>
          <p>{quotes.length} total</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="admin-alert admin-alert-error">{error}</div>}

      {quotes.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="admin-empty">
          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">request_quote</span>
          <h3>No saved quotes</h3>
          <p>Save a cost estimate to keep it here for later.</p>
          <Link href="/cost" className="admin-btn-primary">
            Try the calculator
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="admin-table-wrap">
          <table className="admin-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Estimate</th>
                <th>Status</th>
                <th>Notes</th>
                <th>Date</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {quotes.map((q) => (
                <tr key={q.id}>
                  <td>
                    <strong>
                      {q.currency === "GHS" ? "GHâ‚µ" : q.currency}{" "}
                      {Number(q.estimate).toLocaleString()}
                    </strong>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <StatusBadge variant={quoteVariant(q.status)} />
                  </td>
                  <td>{q.notes ?? "â€”"}</td>
                  <td>{new Date(q.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("en-GB")}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useCart } from "@/lib/cart";
import "@/styles/cart.css";

function currencySymbol(code?: string | null) {
  const c = (code ?? "GHS").toUpperCase();
  if (c === "GHS") return "GH\u20b5";
  if (c === "USD") return "$";
  if (c === "EUR") return "\u20ac";
  if (c === "GBP") return "\u00a3";
  return c;
}

export default function CartPage() {
  const { items, setQuantity, removeFromCart, clear, subtotal } = useCart();

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <section className="cart-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="cart-empty">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">shopping_cart</span>
            <h1>Your cart is empty</h1>
            <p>Browse the shop and add products to get started.</p>
            <Link href="/product" className="cart-empty-cta">
              Browse products
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section className="cart-body">
      <div className="container">
        <nav className="hero-chip-dark" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
          <Link href="/">Home</Link>
          <span className="dot">.</span>
          <span>Cart</span>
        </nav>

        <div className="cart-head">
          <h1>Your cart</h1>
          <button type="button" className="cart-clear" onClick={clear}>
            Clear all
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="cart-layout">
          <div className="cart-items">
            {items.map((item) => {
              const symbol = currencySymbol(item.currency);
              return (
                <div key={item.productId} className="cart-item">
                  <Link href={`/product/${item.slug}`} className="cart-item-image-wrap">
                    {item.image ? (
                      <div
                        className="cart-item-image"
                        style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${item.image}')` }}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="cart-item-image cart-item-image-empty">
                        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">inventory_2</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </Link>

                  <div className="cart-item-info">
                    <Link href={`/product/${item.slug}`} className="cart-item-name">
                      {item.name}
                    </Link>
                    <p className="cart-item-price">
                      {symbol} {Number(item.price).toLocaleString()}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="cart-item-qty">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                      onClick={() => setQuantity(item.productId, item.quantity - 1)}
                      disabled={item.quantity <= 1}
                    >
                      &minus;
                    </button>
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      min={1}
                      value={item.quantity}
                      onChange={(e) => setQuantity(item.productId, Number(e.target.value) || 1)}
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Increase quantity"
                      onClick={() => setQuantity(item.productId, item.quantity + 1)}
                    >
                      +
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="cart-item-subtotal">
                    {symbol} {(Number(item.price) * item.quantity).toLocaleString()}
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="cart-item-remove"
                    aria-label={`Remove ${item.name}`}
                    onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.productId)}
                  >
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined">close</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <aside className="cart-summary">
            <div className="cart-summary-card">
              <h2>Order summary</h2>

              <div className="cart-summary-row">
                <span>Subtotal</span>
                <span>{currencySymbol(items[0]?.currency)} {subtotal.toLocaleString()}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="cart-summary-row">
                <span>Shipping</span>
                <span className="cart-summary-free">Calculated at checkout</span>
              </div>
              <div className="cart-summary-divider" />
              <div className="cart-summary-row cart-summary-total">
                <span>Total</span>
                <span>{currencySymbol(items[0]?.currency)} {subtotal.toLocaleString()}</span>
              </div>

              <Link href="/checkout" className="cart-summary-cta">
                Proceed to checkout
                <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/product" className="cart-summary-ghost">
                Continue shopping
              </Link>

              <p className="cart-summary-note">
                Secure payment via Stripe. Cards and mobile money accepted.
              </p>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: app\(marketing)\checkout\page.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useCart } from "@/lib/cart";
import { clearCart } from "@/modules/cart/service";
import "@/styles/checkout.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

function currencySymbol(code?: string | null) {
  const c = (code ?? "GHS").toUpperCase();
  if (c === "GHS") return "GHÃ¢â€šÂµ";
  if (c === "USD") return "$";
  if (c === "EUR") return "Ã¢â€šÂ¬";
  if (c === "GBP") return "Ã‚Â£";
  return c;
}

export default function CheckoutPage() {
  const { items, subtotal, clear } = useCart();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <section className="checkout-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="cart-empty">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">shopping_cart</span>
            <h1>Nothing to check out</h1>
            <p>Your cart is empty Ã¢â‚¬â€ add some products first.</p>
            <Link href="/product" className="cart-empty-cta">
              Browse products
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const handlePay = async () => {
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/checkout`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        credentials: "include",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          items: items.map((i) => ({
            productId: i.productId,
            quantity: i.quantity,
          })),
          // Stripe will collect real name/email/address during checkout.
          // These placeholders satisfy the backend schema so the request
          // goes through; the webhook updates them after payment.
          shipping: {
            name: "Pending (collected at Stripe)",
            email: "pending@ted-nomex.local",
            phone: null,
            address: "Pending",
            city: "Pending",
            region: null,
            country: "Ghana",
            postalCode: null,
            notes: null,
          },
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.message ?? "Checkout failed");
      }
      if (data.checkoutUrl) {
        clear();
        clearCart();
        window.location.href = data.checkoutUrl;
      } else {
        throw new Error("No checkout URL returned");
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err?.message ?? "Something went wrong");
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section className="checkout-body">
      <div className="container">
        <nav className="hero-chip-dark" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
          <Link href="/">Home</Link>
          <span className="dot">Ã‚Â·</span>
          <Link href="/cart">Cart</Link>
          <span className="dot">Ã‚Â·</span>
          <span>Checkout</span>
        </nav>

        <div className="checkout-head">
          <h1>Checkout</h1>
          <p className="checkout-lead">
            Review your order and continue to our secure payment page.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="checkout-layout">
          <div className="checkout-form">
                        <div className="checkout-section">
              <p className="checkout-kicker">Payment</p>
              <h2>How would you like to pay?</h2>
              <p className="checkout-hint">
                You&apos;ll be redirected to Stripe&apos;s secure checkout to enter your
                email, shipping address, and payment details. Nothing to fill out here.
              </p>

              <div className="checkout-stripe-note">
                <div className="checkout-stripe-note-icon">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">lock</span>
                </div>
                <div className="checkout-stripe-note-body">
                  <strong>Secure payment via Stripe</strong>
                  <p>
                    Cards, mobile money, and Apple Pay accepted. Your payment
                    details never touch our servers â€” Stripe handles them
                    end-to-end.
                  </p>
                  <div className="checkout-stripe-badges">
                    <span className="stripe-badge">
                      <span className="material-symbols-outlined">credit_card</span>
                      Cards
                    </span>
                    <span className="stripe-badge">
                      <span className="material-symbols-outlined">smartphone</span>
                      Mobile money
                    </span>
                    <span className="stripe-badge">
                      <span className="material-symbols-outlined">verified_user</span>
                      PCI-compliant
                    </span>
                    <span className="stripe-badge">
                      <span className="material-symbols-outlined">lock</span>
                      256-bit SSL
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div className="checkout-error">
                <span className="material-symbols-outlined">error</span>
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              className="checkout-submit"
              onClick={handlePay}
              disabled={loading}
            >
              {loading ? "Redirecting to StripeÃ¢â‚¬Â¦" : "Pay with Stripe"}
              {!loading && <span className="material-symbols-outlined">lock</span>}
            </button>

            <Link href="/cart" className="checkout-back">
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_back</span>
              Back to cart
            </Link>
          </div>

          <aside className="checkout-summary">
            <div className="checkout-summary-card">
              <h2>Your order</h2>
              <div className="checkout-summary-items">
                {items.map((item) => {
                  const symbol = currencySymbol(item.currency);
                  return (
                    <div key={item.productId} className="checkout-summary-item">
                      <div className="checkout-summary-thumb">
                        {item.image ? (
                          <div style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${item.image}')` }} />
                        ) : (
                          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">inventory_2</span>
                        )}
                        <span className="checkout-summary-badge">{item.quantity}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="checkout-summary-meta">
                        <p className="checkout-summary-name">{item.name}</p>
                        <p className="checkout-summary-price">
                          {symbol}{" "}
                          {(Number(item.price) * item.quantity).toLocaleString()}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              <div className="cart-summary-divider" />
              <div className="checkout-summary-row">
                <span>Subtotal</span>
                <span>
                  {currencySymbol(items[0]?.currency)}{" "}
                  {subtotal.toLocaleString()}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="checkout-summary-row">
                <span>Shipping</span>
                <span className="cart-summary-free">Calculated at Stripe</span>
              </div>
              <div className="cart-summary-divider" />
              <div className="checkout-summary-row checkout-summary-total">
                <span>Total</span>
                <span>
                  {currencySymbol(items[0]?.currency)}{" "}
                  {subtotal.toLocaleString()}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="checkout-summary-note">
                You&apos;ll be redirected to Stripe to complete payment securely.
              </p>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";

export const metadata = { title: "Order confirmed" };

export default function CheckoutSuccessPage() {
  return (

      <div className="container">
        <div className="checkout-success">
          <div className="checkout-success-icon">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">check_circle</span>
          </div>
          <h1>Thank you â€” order received</h1>

            Payment confirmed. We&apos;ve sent a receipt to your email and our team
            is preparing your order for delivery.
          </p>
          <div className="checkout-success-actions">
            <Link href="/product" className="cart-summary-cta">
              Continue shopping
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
            </Link>
            <Link href="/" className="cart-summary-ghost">
              Back home
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import "@/styles/contact.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

const MAP_EMBED_URL =
  "https://www.google.com/maps/embed?pb=!1m18!1m12!1m3!1d3970.9780867857566!2d-0.19688492497356382!3d5.560283794412008!2m3!1f0!2f0!3f0!3m2!1i1024!2i768!4f13.1!3m3!1m2!1s0x0%3A0x0!2zNcKwMzMnMzcuMCJOIDDCsDExJzQ4LjEiVw!5e0!3m2!1sen!2sgh!4v1730000000000!5m2!1sen!2sgh";

export default function ContactPage() {
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    phone: "",
    subject: "",
    message: "",
  });
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"idle" | "sending" | "sent" | "error">("idle");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const onSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setStatus("sending");
    setError(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/enquiries`, {
        method: "POST",
        credentials: "include",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: form.name.trim(),
          email: form.email.trim().toLowerCase(),
          phone: form.phone.trim() || null,
          subject: form.subject.trim() || null,
          message: form.message.trim(),
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data?.message ?? `Failed (${res.status})`);
      }

      setStatus("sent");
      setForm({ name: "", email: "", phone: "", subject: "", message: "" });
    } catch (err) {
      setStatus("error");
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not send message");
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* â”€â”€ Hero â”€â”€ */}
      <section className="contact-hero">
        <div className="container">
          <nav className="hero-chip" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
            <Link href="/">Home</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <span>Contact</span>
          </nav>
          <p className="hero-eyebrow">Get in touch</p>
          <h1 className="hero-title">
            Let&apos;s build <span>something</span> together.
          </h1>
          <p className="hero-lead">
            Have a question, need a quote, or want to discuss a project? Send us
            a message â€” our team responds within 24 hours.
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* â”€â”€ Body â”€â”€ */}
      <section className="contact-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="contact-layout">

            {/* Form panel */}
            <div className="contact-form-panel">
              <div>
                <h2>Send us a message</h2>
                <p>
                  Fill in the form below and our team will get back to you shortly.
                </p>
              </div>

              <form className="contact-form" onSubmit={onSubmit}>
                <div className="form-field">
                  <label htmlFor="contact-name">
                    Your name <span className="required">*</span>
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="contact-name"
                    type="text"
                    required
                    minLength={2}
                    maxLength={120}
                    value={form.name}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="Kwame Mensah"
                    disabled={status === "sending"}
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="form-field">
                  <label htmlFor="contact-email">
                    Email <span className="required">*</span>
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="contact-email"
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={form.email}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="kwame@example.com"
                    disabled={status === "sending"}
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="form-field">
                  <label htmlFor="contact-phone">Phone (optional)</label>
                  <input
                    id="contact-phone"
                    type="tel"
                    value={form.phone}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, phone: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="+233 ..."
                    disabled={status === "sending"}
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="form-field">
                  <label htmlFor="contact-subject">Subject (optional)</label>
                  <input
                    id="contact-subject"
                    type="text"
                    value={form.subject}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, subject: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="Project enquiry, support, partnershipâ€¦"
                    disabled={status === "sending"}
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="form-field">
                  <label htmlFor="contact-message">
                    Message <span className="required">*</span>
                  </label>
                  <textarea
                    id="contact-message"
                    required
                    value={form.message}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, message: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="Tell us a bit about what you needâ€¦"
                    disabled={status === "sending"}
                  />
                </div>

                {status === "sent" && (
                  <p className="contact-feedback contact-feedback-success">
                    Thanks â€” your message has been sent. We&apos;ll be in touch shortly.
                  </p>
                )}
                {status === "error" && error && (
                  <p className="contact-feedback contact-feedback-error">
                    {error}
                  </p>
                )}

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="contact-submit"
                  disabled={status === "sending"}
                >
                  {status === "sending" ? "Sendingâ€¦" : "Send message"}
                  {status !== "sending" && (
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                  )}
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>

            {/* Sidebar */}
            <aside className="contact-sidebar">
              <div className="contact-info-card">
                <p className="contact-info-kicker">Reach us</p>
                <h3>Contact details</h3>

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import "@/styles/cost.css";

const API = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "http://localhost:4000";

const WALL_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Painting (GHâ‚µ 154)", value: 154 },
  { label: "Painting + Minor Repairs (GHâ‚µ 257)", value: 257 },
  { label: "Hanging Lining Paper (GHâ‚µ 354)", value: 354 },
];

export default function CostPage() {
  const [wallValue, setWallValue] = useState(WALL_OPTIONS[0].value);
  const [area, setArea] = useState(0);
  const [windows, setWindows] = useState(0);
  const [rooms, setRooms] = useState(1);
  const [sqm, setSqm] = useState(0);
  const [floors, setFloors] = useState(1);

  // Save-to-DB form state
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<{ kind: "success" | "error"; text: string } | null>(null);

  const total =
    wallValue + area * 4 + windows * 120 + rooms * 300 + sqm * 6 + floors * 500;

const saveEstimate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  setMessage(null);
  setSaving(true);
  try {
    // Match the backend's inputsSchema exactly:
    //   walls, area, windows, rooms, sqm, floors â€” all numbers
    const payload = {
      name: name.trim(),
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      phone: phone.trim() || null,
      inputs: {
        walls: wallValue,          // â† `walls`, not `wallValue`
        area: Number(area),
        windows: Number(windows),
        rooms: Number(rooms),
        sqm: Number(sqm),
        floors: Number(floors),
      },
      estimate: Number(total),
      currency: "GHS",
      notes: notes.trim() || null,
    };

    // Append the human-readable wall option to notes so admin sees it
    const wallLabel = WALL_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.value === wallValue)?.label ?? "";
    const noteLine = `Wall option: ${wallLabel}`;
    payload.notes = payload.notes ? `${payload.notes}\n\n${noteLine}` : noteLine;

    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/quotes`, {
      method: "POST",
      credentials: "include",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });

    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));

    if (!res.ok) {
      const details =
        data?.errors ??
        data?.issues ??
        data?.message ??
        `Failed (${res.status})`;
      throw new Error(
        typeof details === "string" ? details : JSON.stringify(details)
      );
    }

    setMessage({
      kind: "success",
      text: "Estimate saved. Our team will reach out to confirm.",
    });
    setName("");
    setEmail("");
    setPhone("");
    setNotes("");
  } catch (err) {
    setMessage({
      kind: "error",
      text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not save estimate",
    });
  } finally {
    setSaving(false);
  }
};

  return (
    <div className="cost-page">

      <section className="cost-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="cost-layout">

            {/* â”€â”€ LEFT: Calculator â”€â”€ */}
            <div className="calculator-shell">
              <div>
                <h2>Build your estimate</h2>
                <p>
                  Adjust the sliders and dropdowns below to estimate the cost of
                  your project. Save your estimate on the right to send it to our
                  team.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="calc-row">
                <label>Walls & Ceilings</label>
                <select
                  value={wallValue}
                  onChange={(e) => setWallValue(Number(e.target.value))}
                >
                  {WALL_OPTIONS.map((o) => (
                    <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>
                      {o.label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="calc-row">
                <label>
                  Area to be renovated
                  <span className="calc-value">{area} mÂ²</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={0}
                  max={500}
                  value={area}
                  style={{ ["--fill" as any]: `${(area / 500) * 100}%` }}
                  onChange={(e) => setArea(Number(e.target.value))}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="calc-row">
                <label>
                  Windows to replace
                  <span className="calc-value">{windows}</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={0}
                  max={15}
                  value={windows}
                  style={{ ["--fill" as any]: `${(windows / 15) * 100}%` }}
                  onChange={(e) => setWindows(Number(e.target.value))}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="calc-row">
                <label>Number of rooms</label>
                <select
                  value={rooms}
                  onChange={(e) => setRooms(Number(e.target.value))}
                >
                  {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((r) => (
                    <option key={r} value={r}>
                      Room {r}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="calc-row">
                <label>
                  Square metres
                  <span className="calc-value">{sqm} mÂ²</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={0}
                  max={500}
                  value={sqm}
                  style={{ ["--fill" as any]: `${(sqm / 500) * 100}%` }}
                  onChange={(e) => setSqm(Number(e.target.value))}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="calc-row">
                <label>Number of floors</label>
                <select
                  value={floors}
                  onChange={(e) => setFloors(Number(e.target.value))}
                >
                  {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((f) => (
                    <option key={f} value={f}>
                      {f} floor{f > 1 ? "s" : ""}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="calc-total">
                <p className="calc-total-kicker">Estimated total</p>
                <p className="calc-total-value">
                  <span>GHS</span>
                  {total.toLocaleString()}
                </p>
                <p className="calc-total-note">
                  This is an indicative estimate. Final pricing depends on site
                  inspection, materials, and timeline.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* â”€â”€ RIGHT: Save form â”€â”€ */}
            <aside className="cost-save">
              <p className="cost-save-kicker">Save your estimate</p>
              <h2>Send this estimate to our team</h2>
              <p>
                Fill in your details below and we&apos;ll save your estimate and
                get back to you within 24 hours.
              </p>

              <form onSubmit={saveEstimate} style={{ display: "contents" }}>
                <div className="cost-field">
                  <label htmlFor="cost-name">Full name</label>
                  <input
                    id="cost-name"
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={name}
                    onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Kwame Mensah"
                    minLength={2}
                    maxLength={120}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="cost-field">
                  <label htmlFor="cost-email">Email</label>
                  <input
                    id="cost-email"
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="kwame@example.com"
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="cost-field">
                  <label htmlFor="cost-phone">Phone (optional)</label>
                  <input
                    id="cost-phone"
                    type="tel"
                    value={phone}
                    onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="+233 ..."
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="cost-field">
                  <label htmlFor="cost-notes">Notes (optional)</label>
                  <textarea
                    id="cost-notes"
                    rows={3}
                    value={notes}
                    onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Timeline, location, special requestsâ€¦"
                  />
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="cost-save-cta"
                  disabled={saving}
                >
                  {saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : "Save estimate"}
                  {!saving && (
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                  )}
                </button>

                {message && (
                  <p
                    className={`cost-message cost-message-${message.kind}`}
                    role="alert"
                  >
                    {message.text}
                  </p>
                )}
              </form>
            </aside>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getProperty, listProperties } from "@/modules/estate/service";
import "@/styles/estate.css";
export const instant = false;
export default async function EstateDetailPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const property = await getProperty(id);
  if (!property) return notFound();

  // Fetch related for the bottom section
  let related: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listProperties>> = [];
  try {
    const all = await listProperties();
    related = all.filter((p) => p.id !== property.id).slice(0, 3);
  } catch {}

  return (
    <>
      {/* Hero */}
      <section className="estate-detail-hero">
        <div className="estate-detail-hero-bg" style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${property.images[0] ?? ""}')` }}>
          <div className="estate-detail-hero-overlay" />
        </div>
        <div className="container">
          <nav className="hero-chip" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
            <Link href="/">Home</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <Link href="/estate">Real estate</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <span className="current">{property.title}</span>
          </nav>

          <div className="estate-detail-hero-content">
            <div className="estate-detail-hero-meta">
              <span className="estate-detail-status">{property.status}</span>
              <span className="estate-detail-type">{property.type}</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="estate-detail-title">{property.title}</h1>
            <p className="estate-detail-location">
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">location_on</span>
              {property.location}
            </p>
            <div className="estate-detail-price">
              <span className="estate-detail-price-currency">{property.currency}</span>
              <span className="estate-detail-price-value">
                {Number(property.price).toLocaleString()}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Body */}
      <section className="estate-detail-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="estate-detail-layout">

            {/* Main column */}
            <div className="estate-detail-main">
              {/* Specs strip */}
              <div className="estate-detail-specs">
                <div className="spec">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">bed</span>
                  <span className="spec-value">{property.bedrooms}</span>
                  <span className="spec-label">Bedrooms</span>
                </div>
                <div className="spec">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">bathtub</span>
                  <span className="spec-value">{property.bathrooms}</span>
                  <span className="spec-label">Bathrooms</span>
                </div>
                <div className="spec">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">directions_car</span>
                  <span className="spec-value">{property.parking}</span>
                  <span className="spec-label">Parking</span>
                </div>
                <div className="spec">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">square_foot</span>
                  <span className="spec-value">{property.area}</span>
                  <span className="spec-label">Sqft</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Description */}
              <div className="estate-detail-section">
                <p className="estate-detail-kicker">About the property</p>
                <h2>Property description</h2>
                <p className="estate-detail-desc">{property.description}</p>
              </div>

              {/* Amenities */}
              {property.amenities?.length > 0 && (
                <div className="estate-detail-section">
                  <p className="estate-detail-kicker">What's included</p>
                  <h2>Amenities</h2>
                  <div className="estate-detail-amenities">
                    {property.amenities.map((a) => (
                      <div key={a} className="amenity">
                        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">check_circle</span>
                        {a}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Features / type */}
              <div className="estate-detail-section">
                <p className="estate-detail-kicker">Overview</p>
                <h2>Key details</h2>
                <div className="estate-detail-facts">
                  <div className="fact">
                    <span className="fact-label">Property type</span>
                    <span className="fact-value">{property.type}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="fact">
                    <span className="fact-label">Status</span>
                    <span className="fact-value">{property.status}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="fact">
                    <span className="fact-label">Location</span>
                    <span className="fact-value">{property.location}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="fact">
                    <span className="fact-label">Currency</span>
                    <span className="fact-value">{property.currency}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Sticky sidebar â€” booking */}
            <aside className="estate-detail-sidebar">
              <div className="estate-detail-card">
                <p className="estate-detail-card-kicker">Listed at</p>
                <div className="estate-detail-card-price">
                  <span>{property.currency}</span>
                  {Number(property.price).toLocaleString()}
                </div>

                <div className="estate-detail-card-meta">
                  <div>
                    <span className="meta-label">Type</span>

                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">call</span>
                  Call us
                </Link>

                  A member of our team will respond within 24 hours.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="estate-detail-agent">
                <div className="estate-detail-agent-avatar">T</div>
                <div>
                  <p className="estate-detail-agent-name">Ted-Nomex Realty</p>
                  <p className="estate-detail-agent-role">Sales &amp; lettings</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </aside>
          </div>

          {/* Related */}
          {related.length > 0 && (
            <div className="estate-related">
              <div className="estate-related-head">
                <h2>You may also like</h2>
                <Link href="/estate" className="estate-related-link">
                  View all properties
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                </Link>
              </div>
              <div className="estate-grid">
                {related.map((p) => (
                  <Link
                    key={p.id}
                    href={`/estate/${p.id}`}
                    className="property-card"
                  >
                    <div
                      className="pc-image"
                      style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${p.images[0] ?? ""}')` }}
                    >
                      <span className="pc-status">{p.status}</span>
                      <span className="pc-price-tag">
                        <span className="pc-price-currency">{p.currency}</span>
                        {Number(p.price).toLocaleString()}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="pc-body">
                      <h3>{p.title}</h3>
                      <p className="pc-location">
                        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">location_on</span>
                        {p.location}
                      </p>
                      <div className="pc-specs">
                        <span className="spec">
                          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">bed</span>
                          {p.bedrooms} bd
                        </span>
                        <span className="spec">
                          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">bathtub</span>
                          {p.bathrooms} ba
                        </span>
                        <span className="spec">
                          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">square_foot</span>
                          {p.area} sqft
                        </span>
                      </div>

                        View details

FILE: app\(marketing)\estate\page.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { listProperties } from "@/modules/estate/service";
import "@/styles/estate.css";

type Property = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listProperties>>[number];
type SortKey = "newest" | "price-asc" | "price-desc";

export default function EstatePage() {
  const [all, setAll] = useState<Property[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string>("");
  const [type, setType] = useState<string>("");
  const [minPrice, setMinPrice] = useState("");
  const [maxPrice, setMaxPrice] = useState("");
  const [beds, setBeds] = useState(0);
  const [baths, setBaths] = useState(0);
  const [location, setLocation] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortKey>("newest");

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const data = await listProperties();
        setAll(data);
      } catch (err) {
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not load properties");
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  const types = useMemo(() => {
    const s = new Set<string>();
    all.forEach((p) => p.type && s.add(p.type));
    return Array.from(s);
  }, [all]);

  const locations = useMemo(() => {
    const s = new Set<string>();
    all.forEach((p) => p.location && s.add(p.location));
    return Array.from(s);
  }, [all]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let out = all.filter((p) => {
      if (status && p.status !== status) return false;
      if (type && p.type !== type) return false;
      if (location && p.location !== location) return false;
      if (beds && p.bedrooms < beds) return false;
      if (baths && p.bathrooms < baths) return false;
      if (minPrice && Number(p.price) < Number(minPrice)) return false;
      if (maxPrice && Number(p.price) > Number(maxPrice)) return false;
      return true;
    });
    out = [...out];
    if (sort === "price-asc") out.sort((a, b) => Number(a.price) - Number(b.price));
    if (sort === "price-desc") out.sort((a, b) => Number(b.price) - Number(a.price));
    return out;
  }, [all, status, type, location, beds, baths, minPrice, maxPrice, sort]);

  const reset = () => {
    setStatus(""); setType(""); setLocation("");
    setMinPrice(""); setMaxPrice(""); setBeds(0); setBaths(0);
    setSort("newest");
  };

  const activeFilters =
    (status ? 1 : 0) + (type ? 1 : 0) + (location ? 1 : 0) +
    (minPrice ? 1 : 0) + (maxPrice ? 1 : 0) + (beds ? 1 : 0) + (baths ? 1 : 0);

  return (
    <>
      {/* Hero */}
      <section className="estate-hero">
        <div className="container">
          <nav className="hero-chip" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
            <Link href="/">Home</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <span>Real estate</span>
          </nav>
          <p className="hero-eyebrow">Our real estate</p>
          <h1 className="hero-title">
            Find your next <span>property</span>.
          </h1>
          <p className="hero-lead">
            At Ted-Nomex Construction Company, we extend our expertise beyond
            construction to offer comprehensive real estate services â€” from
            residential homes to commercial and industrial developments across
            Ghana.
          </p>

          <div className="estate-hero-stats">
            <div className="estate-hero-stat">
              <span className="estate-hero-stat-value">{all.length}</span>
              <span className="estate-hero-stat-label">Listings available</span>
            </div>
            <div className="estate-hero-stat">
              <span className="estate-hero-stat-value">{locations.length}</span>
              <span className="estate-hero-stat-label">Locations</span>
            </div>
            <div className="estate-hero-stat">
              <span className="estate-hero-stat-value">12+</span>
              <span className="estate-hero-stat-label">Years in real estate</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Body */}
      <section className="estate-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="layout">

            {/* SIDEBAR */}
            <aside className="estate-sidebar">
              <div className="filter-head">
                <h3>Filters</h3>
                {activeFilters > 0 && (
                  <button type="button" className="filter-reset" onClick={reset}>
                    Reset ({activeFilters})
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="filter-group">
                <p className="group-label">Status</p>
                <div className="filter-checks">
                  {["For Sale", "For Rent"].map((s) => (
                    <label key={s}>
                      <input
                        type="radio"
                        name="status"
                        checked={status === s}
                        onChange={() => setStatus(status === s ? "" : s)}
                      />
                      <span>{s}</span>
                    </label>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="filter-group">
                <p className="group-label">Type</p>
                <div className="filter-checks">
                  {types.length === 0 && <span className="filter-empty">No types yet</span>}
                  {types.map((t) => (
                    <label key={t}>
                      <input
                        type="radio"
                        name="type"
                        checked={type === t}
                        onChange={() => setType(type === t ? "" : t)}
                      />
                      <span>{t}</span>
                    </label>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="filter-group">
                <p className="group-label">Price range</p>
                <div className="filter-price">
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    placeholder="Min"
                    value={minPrice}
                    onChange={(e) => setMinPrice(e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <span>â€”</span>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    placeholder="Max"
                    value={maxPrice}
                    onChange={(e) => setMaxPrice(e.target.value)}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="filter-group">
                <p className="group-label">Bedrooms</p>
                <div className="filter-pills">
                  {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((n) => (
                    <button
                      key={n}
                      type="button"
                      className={beds === n ? "active" : ""}
                      onClick={() => setBeds(n)}
                    >
                      {n === 0 ? "Any" : `${n}+`}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="filter-group">
                <p className="group-label">Bathrooms</p>
                <div className="filter-pills">
                  {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((n) => (
                    <button
                      key={n}
                      type="button"
                      className={baths === n ? "active" : ""}
                      onClick={() => setBaths(n)}
                    >
                      {n === 0 ? "Any" : `${n}+`}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="filter-group">
                <p className="group-label">Location</p>
                <div className="filter-checks">
                  {locations.length === 0 && <span className="filter-empty">No locations yet</span>}
                  {locations.map((l) => (
                    <label key={l}>
                      <input
                        type="radio"
                        name="location"
                        checked={location === l}
                        onChange={() => setLocation(location === l ? "" : l)}
                      />
                      <span>{l}</span>
                    </label>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </aside>

            {/* MAIN */}
            <div className="estate-main">
              <div className="results-head">
                <p>
                  <span>{filtered.length}</span>{" "}
                  {filtered.length === 1 ? "property" : "properties"} available
                </p>
                <div className="results-sort">
                  <label htmlFor="sort">Sort by</label>
                  <select
                    id="sort"
                    value={sort}
                    onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as SortKey)}
                  >
                    <option value="newest">Newest</option>
                    <option value="price-asc">Price: low â†’ high</option>
                    <option value="price-desc">Price: high â†’ low</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {loading ? (
                <div className="estate-loading">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">progress_activity</span>
                  <p>Loading propertiesâ€¦</p>
                </div>
              ) : error ? (
                <div className="estate-empty">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">error</span>
                  <h3>Could not load properties</h3>
                  <p>{error}</p>
                </div>
              ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="estate-empty">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">home_work</span>
                  <h3>No properties match your filters</h3>
                  <p>Try adjusting the filters on the left, or reset them to see all listings.</p>
                  {activeFilters > 0 && (
                    <button type="button" className="estate-empty-cta" onClick={reset}>
                      Clear filters
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="estate-grid">
                  {filtered.map((p) => (
                    <Link
                      key={p.id}
                      href={`/estate/${p.id}`}
                      className="property-card"
                    >
                      <div
                        className="pc-image"
                        style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${p.images[0] ?? ""}')` }}
                      >
                        <span className="pc-status">{p.status}</span>
                        <span className="pc-price-tag">
                          <span className="pc-price-currency">{p.currency}</span>
                          {Number(p.price).toLocaleString()}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="pc-body">
                        <h3>{p.title}</h3>
                        <p className="pc-location">
                          <span className="material-symbols-outlined">location_on</span>
                          {p.location}
                        </p>
                        <div className="pc-specs">
                          <span className="spec">
                            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">bed</span>
                            {p.bedrooms} bd
                          </span>
                          <span className="spec">
                            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">bathtub</span>
                            {p.bathrooms} ba
                          </span>
                          <span className="spec">
                            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">square_foot</span>

                        <span className="pc-link">

      {/* CTA */}

          <div className="estate-cta-text">

              Looking for <span>something specific</span>?

export const instant = false;

import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getNews, listNews } from "@/modules/cms/news/service";
import "@/styles/news.css";

export default async function NewsDetailPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ slug: string }>;
}) {
  const { slug } = await params;

  // Resolve the post by slug â€” works whether getNews returns
  // a single item (by id/slug) or the full list.
  const found = await getNews(slug).catch(() => null);
  const all = await listNews();

  const post =
    found && (found as any).slug === slug
      ? found
      : all.find((p) => p.slug === slug);

  if (!post) return notFound();

  // Related â€” 3 most recent excluding current
  let related: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listNews>> = [];
  try {
    const all = await listNews();
    related = all.filter((p) => p.slug !== post.slug).slice(0, 3);
  } catch {}

  // If your schema has a rich-text/HTML field, swap this for the real one.
  // Fallback uses `excerpt` so the page never breaks.
  const bodyHtml = (post as any).content ?? (post as any).body ?? null;
  const paragraphs: string[] =
    !bodyHtml && (post as any).excerpt
      ? [String((post as any).excerpt)]
      : [];

  return (
    <>
      {/* Hero */}
      <section className="news-detail-hero">
        <div
          className="news-detail-hero-bg"
          style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${post.coverImage}')` }}
        />
        <div className="news-detail-hero-overlay" />
        <div className="container">
          <nav className="hero-chip" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
            <Link href="/">Home</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <Link href="/news">News</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <span className="current">{post.title}</span>
          </nav>

          <div className="news-detail-hero-content">
            <div className="news-detail-hero-meta">
              <span className="news-detail-status">{post.category}</span>
              <span className="news-detail-type">Article</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="news-detail-title">{post.title}</h1>
            <p className="news-detail-meta-line">
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">calendar_today</span>
              {post.publishedAt}
              <span className="dot">Â·</span>
              <span className="material-symbols-outlined">person</span>
              Ted-Nomex Editorial
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Body */}
      <section className="news-detail-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="news-detail-layout">
            {/* Main */}
            <div className="news-detail-main">
              <article className="news-prose">
                {bodyHtml ? (
                  <div dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: bodyHtml }} />
                ) : paragraphs.length > 0 ? (
                  paragraphs.map((p, i) => <p key={i}>{p}</p>)
                ) : (
                  <p>This article is coming soon. Please check back later.</p>
                )}
              </article>
            </div>

            {/* Sidebar */}
            <aside className="news-detail-sidebar">
              <div className="news-detail-card">
                <p className="news-detail-card-kicker">Stay in the loop</p>
                <h3>Get project updates in your inbox.</h3>
                <p>
                  Occasional stories about what we&apos;re building across Ghana â€”
                  no spam, unsubscribe anytime.
                </p>
                <Link href="/contact" className="news-detail-card-cta">
                  Contact us
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                </Link>
                <Link href="/news" className="news-detail-card-cta-ghost">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_back</span>
                  All articles
                </Link>
              </div>

              <div className="news-detail-author">
                <div className="news-detail-author-avatar">T</div>
                <div>
                  <p className="news-detail-author-name">Ted-Nomex Editorial</p>
                  <p className="news-detail-author-role">Newsroom</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </aside>
          </div>

          {/* Related */}
          {related.length > 0 && (
            <div className="news-related">
              <div className="news-related-head">
                <h2>More from the newsroom</h2>
                <Link href="/news" className="news-related-link">
                  View all
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                </Link>
              </div>
              <div className="news-grid">
                {related.map((p) => (
                  <Link
                    key={p.id}
                    href={`/news/${p.slug}`}
                    className="news-card"

                      className="nc-image"

                    >

import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { listNews } from "@/modules/cms/news/service";
import "@/styles/news.css";

export const metadata = { title: "News" };

async function NewsList() {
  const news = await listNews();
  const [featured, ...rest] = news;
  return (
    <section className="news-body">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="layout">
          <div>
            {featured && (
              <article className="news-featured">
                <div className="featured-image" style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${featured.coverImage}')` }}><span className="badge">Featured</span></div>
                <div className="featured-body">
                  <div className="featured-meta"><span className="cat">{featured.category}</span><span>{featured.publishedAt}</span></div>
                  <h2><Link href={`/news/${featured.slug}`}>{featured.title}</Link></h2>
                  <p>{featured.excerpt}</p>
                  <Link href={`/news/${featured.slug}`} className="read-more">Read article<span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span></Link>
                </div>
              </article>
            )}
            <div className="news-grid">
              {rest.map((post) => (
                <Link key={post.id} href={`/news/${post.slug}`} className="news-card">
                  <div className="nc-image" style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${post.coverImage}')` }}><span className="nc-cat">{post.category}</span></div>
                  <div className="nc-body">
                    <div className="nc-meta"><span>{post.category}</span><span>Â·</span><span>{post.publishedAt}</span></div>
                    <h3>{post.title}</h3>
                    <p>{post.excerpt}</p>
                    <span className="nc-link">Read more<span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span></span>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <aside className="news-sidebar">
            <div className="panel"><p className="panel-title">Recent posts</p>{news.slice(0, 4).map((post) => (<Link key={post.id} href={`/news/${post.slug}`} className="post-mini"><img src={post.coverImage} alt="" /><div className="text"><p className="title">{post.title}</p><span className="date"><span className="material-symbols-outlined">calendar_today</span>{post.publishedAt}</span></div></Link>))}</div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

  return (

        <div className="container">

════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: app\(marketing)\product\[slug]\page.tsx
════════════════════════════════════════════
export const instant = false;

import { Suspense } from "react";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getProduct, getRelatedProducts } from "@/modules/shop/products/service";
import { ProductDetail } from "@/components/marketing/shop/ProductDetail";
import "@/styles/product.css";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> };

async function ProductPage({ params }: Props) {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const product = await getProduct(slug);
  if (!product) return notFound();
  let related: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getRelatedProducts>> = [];
  try { related = await getRelatedProducts(slug, 3); } catch {}
  return <ProductDetail product={product} related={related} />;
}

export default function ProductSinglePage({ params }: Props) {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div style={{ padding: 80, textAlign: "center" }}>Loadingâ€¦</div>}>
      <ProductPage params={params} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}
