FILE: app\(marketing)\product\page.tsx
════════════════════════════════════════════
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { listProducts, type Product } from "@/modules/shop/products/service";
import { ProductCard } from "@/components/marketing/shop/ProductCard";
import "@/styles/product.css";

type Sort = "newest" | "price-asc" | "price-desc" | "name";

export default function ProductPage() {
  const [all, setAll] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("");
  const [maxPrice, setMaxPrice] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<Sort>("newest");

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try { setAll(await listProducts()); }
      catch (err: any) { setError(err?.message ?? "Could not load products"); }
      finally { setLoading(false); }
    })();
  }, []);

  const categories = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, number>();
    all.forEach((p) => {
      const c = p.category || "Uncategorized";
      map.set(c, (map.get(c) ?? 0) + 1);
    });
    return Array.from(map.entries()).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  }, [all]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let out = all.filter((p) => {
      if (search) {
        const q = search.toLowerCase();
        const hay = `${p.name} ${p.description} ${p.category ?? ""}`.toLowerCase();
        if (!hay.includes(q)) return false;
      }
      if (category && (p.category || "Uncategorized") !== category) return false;
      if (maxPrice && Number(p.price) > Number(maxPrice)) return false;
      return true;
    });
    out = [...out];
    switch (sort) {
      case "price-asc":  out.sort((a, b) => Number(a.price) - Number(b.price)); break;
      case "price-desc": out.sort((a, b) => Number(b.price) - Number(a.price)); break;
      case "name":       out.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name)); break;
    }
    return out;
  }, [all, search, category, maxPrice, sort]);

  const reset = () => {
    setSearch(""); setCategory(""); setMaxPrice(""); setSort("newest");
  };
  const activeFilters =
    (search ? 1 : 0) + (category ? 1 : 0) + (maxPrice ? 1 : 0);

  return (
    <>
      {/* â”€â”€ Hero â”€â”€ */}
      <section className="product-hero">
        <div className="container">
          <nav className="hero-chip" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
            <Link href="/">Home</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <span>Shop</span>
          </nav>
          <p className="hero-eyebrow">Our products</p>
          <h1 className="hero-title">
            Quality materials, <span>delivered</span>.
          </h1>
          <p className="hero-lead">
            Construction supplies, tools and finishing materials â€” sourced and
            stocked by Ted-Nomex for projects across Ghana.
          </p>
          <div className="product-hero-stats">
            <div className="product-hero-stat">
              <span className="product-hero-stat-value">{all.length}</span>
              <span className="product-hero-stat-label">Products in stock</span>
            </div>
            <div className="product-hero-stat">
              <span className="product-hero-stat-value">{categories.length}</span>
              <span className="product-hero-stat-label">Categories</span>
            </div>
            <div className="product-hero-stat">
              <span className="product-hero-stat-value">12+</span>
              <span className="product-hero-stat-label">Years supplying</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* â”€â”€ Body â”€â”€ */}
      <section className="product-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="layout">

            {/* Sidebar */}
            <aside className="product-sidebar">
              <div className="filter-head">
                <h3>Filters</h3>
                {activeFilters > 0 && (
                  <button type="button" className="filter-reset" onClick={reset}>
                    Reset ({activeFilters})
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="filter-group">
                <p className="group-label">Search</p>
                <input
                  type="search"
                  className="filter-input"
                  placeholder="Search productsâ€¦"
                  value={search}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="filter-group">
                <p className="group-label">Category</p>
                <div className="filter-checks">
                  {categories.length === 0 && (
                    <span className="filter-empty">No categories yet</span>
                  )}
                  {categories.map(([c, n]) => (
                    <label key={c}>
                      <input
                        type="radio"
                        name="category"
                        checked={category === c}
                        onChange={() => setCategory(category === c ? "" : c)}
                      />
                      <span>
                        {c} <em className="filter-count">({n})</em>
                      </span>
                    </label>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="filter-group">
                <p className="group-label">Max price</p>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  className="filter-input"
                  placeholder="e.g. 5000"
                  value={maxPrice}
                  onChange={(e) => setMaxPrice(e.target.value)}
                />
              </div>
            </aside>

            {/* Main */}
            <div className="product-main">
              <div className="results-head">
                <p>
                  <span>{filtered.length}</span>{" "}
                  {filtered.length === 1 ? "product" : "products"} available
                </p>
                <div className="results-sort">
                  <label htmlFor="sort">Sort by</label>
                  <select
                    id="sort"
                    value={sort}
                    onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as Sort)}
                  >
                    <option value="newest">Newest</option>
                    <option value="price-asc">Price: low â†’ high</option>
                    <option value="price-desc">Price: high â†’ low</option>
                    <option value="name">Name: A â†’ Z</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {loading ? (
                <div className="product-loading">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">progress_activity</span>
                  <p>Loading productsâ€¦</p>
                </div>
              ) : error ? (
                <div className="product-empty">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">error</span>
                  <h3>Could not load products</h3>
                  <p>{error}</p>
                </div>
              ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="product-empty">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">inventory_2</span>
                  <h3>No products match your filters</h3>
                  <p>Try adjusting the filters on the left, or reset them to see all products.</p>
                  {activeFilters > 0 && (
                    <button type="button" className="product-empty-cta" onClick={reset}>
                      Clear filters
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="product-grid">
                  {filtered.map((p) => <ProductCard key={p.id} product={p} />)}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* â”€â”€ CTA â”€â”€ */}
      <section className="product-cta">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="product-cta-text">
            <h2>
              Need a <span>bulk quote</span>?
            </h2>
            <p>Tell us the quantities â€” we&apos;ll send pricing and lead times.</p>
          </div>
          <Link href="/contact" className="product-cta-btn">
            Request a quote
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

      "Twelve-storey mixed-use tower with ground-level retail, four floors of offices, and residential apartments above. Post-tensioned slabs keep floor-to-floor heights low.",
    images: [
      "/projects/Architecture2.jpg",
      "/projects/Architecture4.jpg",
      "/projects/Architecture1.jpg",
    ],
  },
];

const CATEGORIES: { label: string; value: Category }[] = [
  { label: "All", value: "all" },
  { label: "Renovation", value: "renovate" },
  { label: "Building", value: "building" },
  { label: "Architecture", value: "architecture" },
];

const TABS: { label: string; value: Tab; icon: string }[] = [
  { label: "Gallery", value: "gallery", icon: "photo_library" },
  { label: "Description", value: "description", icon: "description" },
  { label: "Details", value: "details", icon: "list_alt" },
  { label: "Progress & Location", value: "progress", icon: "insights" },
];

export default function ProjectsPage() {
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<Category>("all");
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("gallery");
  const [showLocation, setShowLocation] = useState(false);
  const [modalProjectId, setModalProjectId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [modalImageIndex, setModalImageIndex] = useState(0);

  const modalProject = useMemo(
    () => PROJECTS.find((p) => p.id === modalProjectId) ?? null,
    [modalProjectId]
  );

  const filtered = useMemo(
    () =>
      category === "all"
        ? PROJECTS
        : PROJECTS.filter((p) => p.category === category),
    [category]
  );

  const openModal = (id: number, imageIndex = 0) => {
    setModalProjectId(id);
    setModalImageIndex(imageIndex);
  };
  const closeModal = () => setModalProjectId(null);

  const nextImage = () => {
    if (!modalProject) return;
    setModalImageIndex((i) => (i + 1) % modalProject.images.length);
  };
  const prevImage = () => {
    if (!modalProject) return;
    setModalImageIndex(
      (i) => (i - 1 + modalProject.images.length) % modalProject.images.length
    );
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (modalProjectId === null) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") closeModal();
      if (e.key === "ArrowRight") nextImage();
      if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") prevImage();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [modalProjectId, modalProject]);

  const groupedCounts = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      all: PROJECTS.length,
      renovate: PROJECTS.filter((p) => p.category === "renovate").length,
      building: PROJECTS.filter((p) => p.category === "building").length,
      architecture: PROJECTS.filter((p) => p.category === "architecture").length,
    } as Record<Category, number>;
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      {/* HERO */}
      <section className="projects-hero">
        <div
          className="projects-hero-bg"
          style={{ backgroundImage: "url('/projects/Projects-banner.jpg')" }}
        />
        <div className="projects-hero-overlay" />
        <div className="projects-hero-content">
          <nav className="hero-chip" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
            <Link href="/">Home</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <Link href="/projects">Portfolio</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <span className="current">Gallery</span>
          </nav>
          <p className="hero-eyebrow">Our portfolio</p>
          <h1 className="hero-title">
            Work we&apos;re <span>proud</span> to sign.
          </h1>
          <p className="hero-lead">
            Residential, commercial, and industrial projects across Ghana.
            Every build is a case study in craft, planning, and follow-through.
          </p>

          <div className="projects-hero-stats">
            <div className="stat">
              <span className="stat-value">{PROJECTS.length}</span>
              <span className="stat-label">Featured projects</span>
            </div>
            <div className="stat">
              <span className="stat-value">{groupedCounts.architecture}</span>
              <span className="stat-label">Architectural builds</span>
            </div>
            <div className="stat">
              <span className="stat-value">{groupedCounts.renovate}</span>
              <span className="stat-label">Renovations</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* BODY */}
      <section className="projects-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="projects-layout">
            {/* SIDEBAR */}
            <aside className="projects-sidebar">
              <div className="sidebar-block">
                <h2 className="sidebar-title">Sections</h2>
                <nav className="sidebar-tabs">
                  {TABS.map((t) => (
                    <button
                      key={t.value}
                      type="button"
                      className={`sidebar-tab ${tab === t.value ? "active" : ""}`}
                      onClick={() => setTab(t.value)}
                    >
                      <span className="material-symbols-outlined">{t.icon}</span>
                      <span>{t.label}</span>
                      <span className="material-symbols-outlined arrow">
                        chevron_right
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </nav>
              </div>

              <div className="sidebar-block">
                <h2 className="sidebar-title">Brochures</h2>
                <div className="sidebar-brochures">
                  <a
                    href="/brochures/ted-nomex-portfolio.pdf"
                    className="brochure-btn"
                    download
                  >
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined">download</span>
                    <span className="label">
                      Portfolio
                      <small>PDF Â· 4.2 MB</small>
                    </span>
                  </a>
                  <a
                    href="/brochures/ted-nomex-brochure.pdf"
                    className="brochure-btn"
                    download
                  >
                    <span className="material-symbols-outlined">description</span>
                    <span className="label">
                      Company brochure
                      <small>PDF Â· 1.8 MB</small>
                    </span>
                  </a>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="sidebar-block sidebar-contact">
                <p className="sidebar-kicker">Want something like this?</p>
                <h3>Talk to our project team.</h3>
                <Link href="/contact" className="sidebar-cta">
                  Contact us
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                </Link>
              </div>
            </aside>

            {/* MAIN CONTENT */}
            <div className="projects-main">
              {/* GALLERY TAB */}
              {tab === "gallery" && (
                <div className="panel">
                  <div className="panel-head">
                    <div>
                      <p className="panel-kicker">Selected work</p>
                      <h2>Project gallery</h2>
                    </div>

                      {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (

                          key={c.value}

                          className={category === c.value ? "active" : ""}
                          onClick={() => setCategory(c.value)}
                        >
                          {c.label}
                          <span>{groupedCounts[c.value]}</span>

                      ))}
                    </div>

            >

            <div className="modal-image">

              <span className={`modal-cat cat-${modalProject.category}`}>

              <h2 className="modal-title">{modalProject.title}</h2>

                  <span className="meta-value">{modalProject.size}</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="meta-label">Client</span>
                  <span className="meta-value">{modalProject.client}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <p className="modal-desc">{modalProject.description}</p>

              {modalProject.images.length > 1 && (
                <div className="modal-strip">
                  {modalProject.images.map((img, i) => (
                    <button
                      key={img}
                      type="button"
                      className={`strip-thumb ${i === modalImageIndex ? "active" : ""}`}

                      style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${img}')` }}
                      aria-label={`View image ${i + 1}`}
                    />
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}

              <Link
                href="/contact"
                className="modal-cta"
                onClick={closeModal}
              >
                Start a similar project
                <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

export const instant = false;

import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getService, listServices } from "@/modules/cms/services/service";
import "@/styles/services.css";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> };

type ServiceRow = NonNullable<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getService>>>;

export default async function ServiceSinglePage({ params }: Props) {
  const { slug } = await params;

  let service: ServiceRow | null = null;
  try {
    service = await getService(slug);
  } catch {
    service = null;
  }
  if (!service) return notFound();

  let others: ServiceRow[] = [];
  try {
    const all = await listServices();
    others = all.filter((s) => s.slug !== service!.slug).slice(0, 6);
  } catch {
    others = [];
  }

  const cover = service.coverImage ?? "";
  const hasCover = cover.length > 0;

  return (
    <>
      {/* â”€â”€ Hero â”€â”€ */}
      <section className="svc-detail-hero">
        <div
          className="svc-detail-hero-bg"
          style={hasCover ? { backgroundImage: `url('${cover}')` } : undefined}
        />
        <div className="svc-detail-hero-overlay" />
        <div className="container">
          <nav className="hero-chip" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
            <Link href="/">Home</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <Link href="/services">Services</Link>
            <span className="dot">Â·</span>
            <span className="current">{service.title}</span>
          </nav>

          <div className="svc-detail-hero-content">
            {service.tags?.length > 0 && (
              <div className="svc-detail-hero-tags">
                {service.tags.map((t: string) => (
                  <span key={t} className="svc-detail-tag">{t}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
            <h1 className="svc-detail-title">{service.title}</h1>
            <p className="svc-detail-excerpt">{service.excerpt}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* â”€â”€ Body â”€â”€ */}
      <section className="svc-detail-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="svc-detail-layout">
            {/* Main column */}
            <div className="svc-detail-main">
              {service.content && (
                <div className="svc-detail-section">
                  <p className="svc-detail-kicker">About this service</p>
                  <h2>Overview</h2>
                  <p className="svc-detail-desc">{service.content}</p>
                </div>
              )}

              {service.features?.length > 0 && (
                <div className="svc-detail-section">
                  <p className="svc-detail-kicker">What&apos;s included</p>
                  <h2>Key features</h2>
                  <div className="svc-features">
                    {service.features.map((f: string) => (
                      <div key={f} className="svc-feature">
                        <span className="svc-feature-icon">check_circle</span>
                        <span className="svc-feature-text">{f}</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {service.bullets?.length > 0 && (
                <div className="svc-detail-section">
                  <p className="svc-detail-kicker">Deliverables</p>
                  <h2>What you&apos;ll get</h2>
                  <ul className="svc-bullets">
                    {service.bullets.map((b: string) => (
                      <li key={b} className="svc-bullet">
                        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_right</span>
                        {b}
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Sticky sidebar */}
            <aside className="svc-detail-sidebar">
              <div className="svc-detail-card">
                <p className="svc-detail-card-kicker">Get started</p>
                <h3>Request a tailored quote for this service.</h3>
                <p>
                  Tell us about your project and we&apos;ll respond with scope,
                  timeline, and pricing within 24 hours.
                </p>
                <Link href="/contact" className="svc-detail-card-cta">
                  Request a quote
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                </Link>
                <Link href="/services" className="svc-detail-card-cta-ghost">
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_back</span>
                  All services

              </div>

              {others.length > 0 && (
                <div className="panel">
                  <p className="panel-title">Other services</p>
                  <div className="nav-list">
                    {others.map((s: ServiceRow) => (
                      <Link
                        key={s.id}
                        href={`/services/${s.slug}`}
                        className="nav-link"
                      >
                        {s.title}
                        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">chevron_right</span>
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </aside>
          </div>

          {/* Related (bottom grid) */}
          {others.length > 0 && (
            <div className="svc-related">
              <div className="svc-related-head">
                <h2>Explore other services</h2>
                <Link href="/services" className="svc-related-link">
                  View all
                  <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                </Link>
              </div>
              <div className="services-grid-v2">
                {others.slice(0, 3).map((s: ServiceRow, i: number) => (
                  <article key={s.id} className="svc-card">

                      className={s.coverImage ? "svc-image" : "svc-image svc-image-empty"}
                      style={s.coverImage ? { backgroundImage: `url('${s.coverImage}')` } : undefined}
                    >
                      <span className="svc-num">
                        {String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}.
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="svc-body">
                      {s.tags?.length > 0 && (
                        <div className="svc-tags">
                          {s.tags.slice(0, 3).map((t: string) => (
                            <span key={t} className="svc-tag">{t}</span>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <h3>{s.title}</h3>
                      <p>{s.excerpt}</p>
                      <Link href={`/services/${s.slug}`} className="svc-link">
                        Read more
                        <span className="material-symbols-outlined">arrow_forward</span>
                      </Link>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { listServices } from "@/modules/cms/services/service";
import "@/styles/services.css";

export const metadata = { title: "Our Services" };

async function ServicesList() {
  const services = await listServices();

  if (services.length === 0) {
    return (
      <section className="services-body">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="svc-empty">
            <span className="material-symbols-outlined">handyman</span>
            <h3>No services yet</h3>
            <p>Our team is preparing service listings. Check back soon.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section className="services-body">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="layout">
          <div className="services-grid-v2">
            {services.map((service, i) => (
              <article key={service.id} className="svc-card">
                <div
                  className={service.coverImage ? "svc-image" : "svc-image svc-image-empty"}
                  style={service.coverImage ? { backgroundImage: `url('${service.coverImage}')` } : undefined}
                >
                  <span className="svc-num">
                    {String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}.
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="svc-body">
                  {service.tags?.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="svc-tags">
                      {service.tags.slice(0, 3).map((t) => (
                        <span key={t} className="svc-tag">{t}</span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <h3>{service.title}</h3>
                  <p>{service.excerpt}</p>
                  <Link href={`/services/${service.slug}`} className="svc-link">
                    Read more
